**[00:11:09.070] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000062)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:11:11.170] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000063)  
Isang mga kalikasang umaga po ah to the Chair and the Honorable Members of the Committee.

**[00:11:16.950] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000064)  
With your permission, and with the Committee's kind indulgence, allow me to present the

**[00:11:22.710] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000065)  
key highlights of our proposed fiscal year 2027 budget and strategic priorities.

**[00:11:28.230] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000066)  
For a more detailed discussion of these highlights, I respectfully refer the Honorable Members

**[00:11:33.570] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000067)  
to the printed copies that have been distributed for your reference.

**[00:11:38.250] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000068)  
The DENR respectfully requests for a total budget of 28.04 billion pesos.

**[00:11:44.010] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000069)  
This amount covers the DENR Central Office, our 17 regional offices, all staff and line bureaus,

**[00:11:52.150] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000070)  
and attached agencies.

**[00:11:54.330] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000071)  
With an immense mandate covering 15.8 million hectares of classified forest lands and over

**[00:11:59.870] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000072)  
36,000 kilometers of coastline, the Department is entrusted with a challenging, yet vital

**[00:12:05.910] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000073)  
responsibility.

**[00:12:07.810] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000074)  
Our primary priority is translating this mandate into direct community impact.

**[00:12:12.530] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000075)  
Amid limitations in fiscal space, we commit to channeling our resources toward three highly

**[00:12:19.310] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000076)  
impactful pillars.

**[00:12:20.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000081)  
First, we are unlocking our natural capital to drive sustainable socio-economic development.

**[00:12:27.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000082)  
We are transforming the National Greening Program into a forest landscape restoration

**[00:12:32.630] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000083)  
approach focusing on priority landscapes such as the Sierra Madre Protected Landscape.

**[00:12:39.270] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000084)  
For 2027, we aim to support community-based social enterprises, ensuring that we do not

**[00:12:45.650] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000085)  
merely plant trees, but restore biodiversity, strengthen ecosystem resilience, and create

**[00:12:51.910] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000086)  
sustainable livelihoods for local communities.

**[00:12:55.170] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000087)  
We are aggressively scaling up our land titling program.

**[00:12:58.530] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000088)  
Our goal is to issue up to 43,000 land patents in 2027, providing absolute tenure security

**[00:13:05.430] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000089)  
that unlocks vital economic capital for families and households.

**[00:13:10.490] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000090)  
In the mining sector, we are driving towards a 2% mining GDP contribution by 2028.

**[00:13:16.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000091)  
We will maximize our mineral wealth by empowering small-scale miners through the Minahang Bayan Program

**[00:13:23.070] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000098)  
and fast-tracking critical mineral quadrangle mapping, balanced

**[00:13:27.010] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000099)  
strictly with rigorous regulatory compliance monitoring and enforcement.

**[00:13:32.410] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000100)  
Second, we are advancing a clean and healthy environment.

**[00:13:36.590] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000101)  
Reliable access to clean water is a fundamental right.

**[00:13:39.770] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000102)  
For 2027, we are providing 124 million pesos in direct financial assistance to local water

**[00:13:47.330] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000103)  
districts.

**[00:13:47.710] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000104)  
to expand services to isolated and unserved barangays.

**[00:13:52.670] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000106)  
On solid waste management, we continue to empower the local government units and provide them with technical assistance.

**[00:13:59.850] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000107)  
Furthermore, our implementation of the Extended Producer Responsibility Act

**[00:14:04.330] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000108)  
has already mobilized over 1,000 enterprises, diverting 244 million kilograms of waste packaging footprint.

**[00:14:13.850] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000109)  
As part of this effort, we shall aggressively continue our revitalization of major urban waterways,

**[00:14:20.810] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000110)  
particularly the Manila Bay, Pasig River system, and Laguna Lake.

**[00:14:25.430] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000111)  
Third, we are strengthening ecosystem integrity and climate resilience.

**[00:14:29.450] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000112)  
Through the Mines and Geosciences Bureau, we are operating a 24-7 geohazards operations center that sends direct,

**[00:14:37.370] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000113)  
real-time threat advisories to LGUs to protect at-risk barangays.

**[00:14:43.250] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000114)  
We are specifically targeting our interventions to the 18 high-exposure

**[00:14:48.030] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000115)  
provinces identified in the National Adaptation Plan to support risk-informed planning.

**[00:14:53.650] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000116)  
Because ecosystems are our strongest natural defense, we are strengthening the active management

**[00:14:59.950] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000117)  
of our 121 legislated protected areas and our marine protected area networks.

**[00:15:06.870] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000118)  
Fundamental to all these priorities is the paradigm shift brought about by the PENCAS law. The department is currently enhancing its technical capacity and workforce to establish site-specific ecosystem accounts. By measuring the economic value of our environment, the ecosystem services, and the tangible benefits of conservation, we are enabling smarter, data-driven planning, programming, and budgeting.

**[00:15:33.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000119)  
To conclude, Mr. Chair, Honorable Members of the Committee, financing the environment and natural resources is a strategic investment toward achieving nature-positive, climate-resilient, and sustainable Filipino communities and livelihoods. We seek your support in passing the budget. Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

i-discuss ano ang mandato

**[00:16:53.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000146)  
at proseso ng DENR

**[00:16:56.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000147)

**[00:17:08.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000149)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:17:12.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000150)  
Pagdating po sa

**[00:17:14.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000151)

**[00:17:18.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000152)

**[00:17:21.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000153)

**[00:17:22.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000154)

**[00:17:24.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000155)

**[00:17:27.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000156)  
Dumadaan po ito

**[00:17:28.620] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000157)

**[00:17:29.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000158)

**[00:17:39.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000162)

**[00:17:40.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000163)

**[00:17:49.380] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000167)

**[00:17:50.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000168)

**[00:17:53.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000169)

**[00:17:54.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000170)

**[00:19:06.960] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000207)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:19:09.600] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000208)  
Good morning po, Your Honor,

**[00:19:11.680] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000209)  
and to all members of the committee.

**[00:19:17.560] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000210)  
To respond,

**[00:19:19.960] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000211)  
the DENR's budget for a land survey

**[00:19:25.300] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000212)  
as stated to resolve the disputes

**[00:19:28.300] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000213)  
are not enough to implement all the land disputes

**[00:19:35.100] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000214)  
to resolve the survey disputes

**[00:19:39.300] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000215)  
relating to survey.

**[00:19:40.460] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000216)  
There are many types of disputes on land.

**[00:19:47.180] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000217)  
Kasama na yung survey matters.

**[00:19:49.980] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000218)  
So, the land cases that are handled by the DENR

**[00:19:55.320] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000219)  
in our regional offices, Senros and Penros,

**[00:19:58.780] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000220)  
there is a budget for dispute resolution

**[00:20:02.840] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000221)  
like the claims and conflicts

**[00:20:06.040] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000222)  
and alternative dispute resolution

**[00:20:08.040] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000223)  
process.

**[00:20:09.940] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000224)  
But the budget given is meager

**[00:20:14.400] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000225)  
to resolve all the cases presented to us.

**[00:20:18.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000226)  
Okay po, maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[00:20:21.630] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000227)  
So, naintindihan po natin,

**[00:20:23.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000228)  
nakulang yung budget para po sa survey.

**[00:20:25.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000229)  
Second question,

**[00:20:27.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000230)  
dahil nalaman niyo po na kulang,

**[00:20:29.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000231)  
nade-determine po ba ng DENR

**[00:20:31.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000232)  
kung nagre-request ng pondo

**[00:20:34.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000233)  
ang mga provincial officers natin?

**[00:20:38.320] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000234)  
Patungkol po dito sa survey, Mr. Chair?

**[00:20:42.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000235)  
DNR? May answer?

**[00:20:44.600] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000236)  
Sir?

**[00:20:45.840] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000237)  
Yes po, Sir.

**[00:20:47.860] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000238)  
Per request basis po,

**[00:20:50.280] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000239)  
yung ating mga LGUs and applicants,

**[00:20:53.480] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000240)  
yung mga clients po natin,

**[00:20:55.540] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000241)  
they request the DENR

**[00:20:58.040] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000242)  
for a resolution of these conflicts

**[00:21:00.640] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000243)  
and as they are received,

**[00:21:03.700] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000244)  
we determine kung mapupondohan po.

**[00:21:06.940] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000245)  
So, in accordance po dun sa budget na binibigay.

**[00:21:09.480] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000246)  
But we do our best po na makapag-implement

**[00:21:14.720] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000247)  
depending on the budget that you are given to us.

**[00:21:17.300] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000248)  
So, madalas po,

**[00:21:18.100] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000249)  
nagre-request po kami sa central office

**[00:21:22.280] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000250)  
for fund po.

**[00:21:23.600] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000251)  
If hindi po napondohan

**[00:21:26.020] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000252)  
doon sa respective regions

**[00:21:27.500] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000253)  
and penros and senros.

**[00:21:29.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000254)  
Mr. Chair, just a clarification.

**[00:21:32.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000255)  
Please go ahead.

**[00:21:32.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000256)  
Ang pondo po ba ay nakalagak sa regional office

**[00:21:36.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000257)  
o sa provincial office

**[00:21:38.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000258)  
pertaining to survey?

**[00:21:41.740] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000259)  
It's with the regional offices po.

**[00:21:44.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000260)  
Okay, Mr. Chair.

**[00:21:45.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000261)  
Ang punto ko po kung bakit ko tinatanong to Mr. Chair,

**[00:21:49.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000262)  
eh ang dami pong mga application for survey

**[00:21:52.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000263)  
hindi tinutugunan ng DENR.

**[00:21:56.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000264)  
Ang sasabihin ng provincial,

**[00:21:58.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000265)  
wala silang pondo.

**[00:22:01.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000266)  
Ngayon, sa halip na mag-request yung provincial,

**[00:22:04.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000267)  
eh wala pong request na nanggagaling

**[00:22:07.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000268)  
mula sa mga probinsya.

**[00:22:08.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000269)  
In fact, inilalagak lahat doon sa regional.

**[00:22:11.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000270)  
Malaki po ang problema niyan, Mr. Chair.

**[00:22:13.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000271)  
Bakit po?

**[00:22:14.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000272)  
Kasi yan yung nagpapatagal

**[00:22:16.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000273)  
doon sa mga survey request.

**[00:22:19.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000274)  
Sa halip na diretsyo na sa provincial,

**[00:22:22.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000275)  
dadaan pa doon sa regional.

**[00:22:23.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000276)  
Kaya ang mangyayari,

**[00:22:25.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000277)  
binabato-bato ngayon yung mga magsasaka.

**[00:22:28.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000278)  
Ang gagawin, punta ka muna sa

**[00:22:30.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000279)  
menro.

**[00:22:32.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000280)  
Tapos, pagaling sa menro,

**[00:22:35.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000281)  
penro.

**[00:22:36.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000282)  
Tapos, ibabato sa regional office.

**[00:22:39.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000283)  
At pabalik, Mr. Chair,

**[00:22:42.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000284)  
eh ang dami-daming request.

**[00:22:44.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000285)  
So, pwede po bang bigyang kalinawa

**[00:22:47.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000286)  
ng DANR, yung committee,

**[00:22:49.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000287)  
kung bakit ganito po ang siste?

**[00:22:50.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000288)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[00:22:52.640] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000289)  
can you kindly respond

**[00:22:55.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000290)  
to the very

**[00:22:56.920] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000291)  
important question by our

**[00:22:59.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000292)  
minority member?

**[00:23:04.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000293)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:23:06.380] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000294)  
Actually, kung meron po mga request

**[00:23:08.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000295)  
ng region, Mr. Chair,

**[00:23:11.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000296)  
and

**[00:23:11.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000297)  
the finance office,

**[00:23:14.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000298)  
the central office,

**[00:23:15.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000299)  
sees that there is available funding.

**[00:23:19.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000300)  
Binibigay naman po namin, Mr. Chair.

**[00:23:20.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000301)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[00:23:22.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000302)  
hindi po yan ang nangyayari sa ground.

**[00:23:26.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000303)  
Alam niyo po kung anong ginagawa

**[00:23:27.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000304)  
sa mga magsasaka?

**[00:23:28.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000305)  
Sila pa ang sinasabihan ng DANR.

**[00:23:30.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000306)  
Kayo ang maghanap ng surveyor nyo.

**[00:23:33.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000307)  
Private surveyor.

**[00:23:35.510] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000308)  
Kasi sasabihin, walang budget

**[00:23:36.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000309)  
ng DANR.

**[00:23:41.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000310)  
I'd like to defer to Yusek

**[00:23:43.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000311)  
Ernesto Adobo Jr.

**[00:23:45.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000312)  
Yusek Adobo, you have the floor.

**[00:23:47.040] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000313)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:23:48.100] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000314)  
Magandang umaga po sa lahat ng

**[00:23:50.580] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000315)  
kagalang-galang na member

**[00:23:53.060] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000316)  
committee.

**[00:23:54.220] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000317)  
Mr. Chair, this is how we

**[00:23:57.040] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000318)  
actually do the targeting.

**[00:23:59.680] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000319)  
Like what we're doing

**[00:24:01.300] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000320)  
today. We set

**[00:24:03.220] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000321)  
targets.

**[00:24:05.940] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000322)  
We allocate

**[00:24:06.840] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000323)  
amount.

**[00:24:09.180] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000324)  
So, ito,

**[00:24:10.700] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000325)  
cascaded to the

**[00:24:12.900] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000326)  
Penrose. Penrose is

**[00:24:14.700] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000327)  
ang pinakahuling

**[00:24:16.280] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000328)  
allocation ng pwede.

**[00:24:18.720] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000329)  
In draw or the RDS?

**[00:24:21.180] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000330)  
Sa RDS, sir.

**[00:24:21.920] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000331)  
The money

**[00:24:24.900] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000332)  
goes directly

**[00:24:25.920] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000333)  
what is allocated to

**[00:24:28.100] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000334)  
the Penrose.

**[00:24:32.100] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000335)  
Ito yung proseso.

**[00:24:33.380] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000336)  
Pag na-set na yung

**[00:24:34.580] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000337)  
targets, kung ano yung

**[00:24:37.360] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000338)  
allocated for that particular

**[00:24:38.800] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000339)  
activity, spread all over

**[00:24:41.120] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000340)  
the country,

**[00:24:42.240] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000341)  
nakatuka na po.

**[00:24:46.040] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000342)  
Alimbawa, sa

**[00:24:46.920] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000343)  
panggaba, kung saan,

**[00:24:49.240] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000344)  
kung meron silang ganong amount,

**[00:24:51.640] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000345)  
merong corresponding target

**[00:24:53.500] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000346)  
na ginagawa, including the survey,

**[00:24:56.180] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000347)  
tsaka hanggang approval na yun.

**[00:24:58.400] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000348)  
Ang nangyayari po,

**[00:24:59.820] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000349)  
hindi sapat yung

**[00:25:01.720] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000350)  
naibibigay. As much as we wanted.

**[00:25:04.160] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000351)  
Anaman ang

**[00:25:04.760] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000352)  
gustong malaman namin dito

**[00:25:07.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000353)  
sa committee, ay yung

**[00:25:09.580] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000354)  
hinihingi nyo ngayon dito sa budget

**[00:25:11.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000355)  
na ito, alam ba ninyo yan?

**[00:25:14.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000356)  
Meron bang feedback on the ground?

**[00:25:15.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000357)  
Na lahat ng mga

**[00:25:17.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000358)  
nagre-request ng land surveys

**[00:25:19.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000359)  
ay matutugunahan.

**[00:25:21.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000360)  
Kasi, in particular,

**[00:25:23.640] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000361)  
doon siguro sa mga kumakausap

**[00:25:26.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000362)  
kay

**[00:25:26.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000363)  
His Honorable

**[00:25:29.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000364)  
San Fernando,

**[00:25:34.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000365)  
mayroong mga nagre-reklamo

**[00:25:36.140] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000366)  
sa kanya. So, dapat

**[00:25:37.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000367)  
natin malaman na kung yung hinihingi

**[00:25:40.100] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000368)  
ninyo ngayon dito ay kulang

**[00:25:42.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000369)  
para mag-move si

**[00:25:43.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000370)  
Congressman

**[00:25:44.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000371)  
for additional budget.

**[00:25:47.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000372)  
Kasi, baka

**[00:25:48.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000373)  
pabalik-balik lang yung

**[00:25:50.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000374)  
kawawa naman yung kababayan natin

**[00:25:53.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000375)  
na pabalik-balik lang yun na ayaw.

**[00:25:55.380] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000376)  
Yes. Tama po,

**[00:25:57.360] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000377)  
Mr. Chair. Alam nyo po kasi,

**[00:25:58.740] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000378)  
sa ibang programs ng DNR,

**[00:26:01.140] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000379)  
may mga provincial allocation.

**[00:26:03.360] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000380)  
Ito pong sa survey,

**[00:26:04.520] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000381)  
at yun doon sa mga agrarian reform

**[00:26:07.140] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000382)  
matters, lahat nandun sa RD.

**[00:26:09.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000383)  
Kasi, nangyayari po sa ground

**[00:26:11.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000384)  
dito, Mr. Chair, ayun nga,

**[00:26:13.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000385)  
iba-bato-bato

**[00:26:14.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000386)  
palagi yung mga magsasaka,

**[00:26:15.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000387)  
laging walang budget sa provincial.

**[00:26:18.660] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000388)  
Meron po kasi, if I may, Mr. Chair,

**[00:26:20.840] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000389)  
dugtong yung aking statement.

**[00:26:23.740] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000390)  
Pagka na-set yung

**[00:26:25.280] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000391)  
targets, na-allocate yung

**[00:26:27.120] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000392)  
budget for that particular year.

**[00:26:30.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000393)  
Sandali lang.

**[00:26:31.100] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000394)  
Sandali lang, Yusik Adobo.

**[00:26:32.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000395)  
Pag nag-set kayo ng target,

**[00:26:34.740] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000396)  
yun ba ay reports galing on the ground?

**[00:26:36.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000397)  
Baka kayo-kayo lang dito sa table ninyo

**[00:26:38.740] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000398)  
sa Manila, na nag-set ng

**[00:26:40.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000399)  
target?

**[00:26:41.400] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000400)  
In default, Mr. Chair.

**[00:26:42.840] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000401)  
Request yan on...

**[00:26:44.880] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000402)  
Lahat ng region nagre-request.

**[00:26:47.100] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000403)  
Inoconsolidate ninyo.

**[00:26:48.900] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000404)  
Humihingi kayo ng tama.

**[00:26:50.860] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000405)

**[00:26:58.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000407)

**[00:27:01.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000408)

**[00:27:03.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000409)

**[00:27:08.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000412)

**[00:27:09.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000413)

**[00:27:12.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000414)

**[00:27:13.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000415)

**[00:27:15.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000416)

**[00:27:16.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000417)

**[00:27:19.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000418)

**[00:27:21.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000419)

**[00:27:25.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000422)

**[00:27:41.160] DENR Undersecretary Ernesto D. Adobo Jr.** (asr-000427)

**[00:27:44.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000429)

**[00:30:08.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000500)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Thank you, Congressman Rufus.

**[00:30:10.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000501)  
Ayos na?

**[00:30:12.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000502)  
Another one?

**[00:30:13.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000503)  
Meron pa po, Mr. Chair.

**[00:30:14.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000504)  
Sige, you have five minutes, almost up.

**[00:30:16.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000505)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:30:18.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000506)  
Puwede po bang i-discuss ng DENR, Mr. Secretary,

**[00:30:21.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000507)  
ano naman po yung mandato ninyo at proseso pagdating sa foreshore lands at fisher folk tenure?

**[00:30:31.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-000508)  
Mr. Chair, I may defer to the Land Management Bureau.

**[00:30:35.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000509)  
Please go ahead.

**[00:30:36.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000510)  
Land Management Bureau.

**[00:30:40.580] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000511)  
Where are you?

**[00:30:46.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000512)  
Attorney?

**[00:30:47.820] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000513)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:30:48.340] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000514)  
Attorney, ikaw na naman.

**[00:30:51.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000515)  
Bilisan mo na lang.

**[00:30:52.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000516)  
Yung answer kailangan brief, concise, direct to the point.

**[00:30:56.300] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000517)  
Yes, sir. For foreshore areas,

**[00:30:59.220] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000518)  
ang proseso po natin, it is disposed through lease.

**[00:31:04.220] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000519)  
We call it foreshore lease.

**[00:31:05.920] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000520)  
And we have also requirements and procedures for that.

**[00:31:10.640] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000521)  
And the tenure is foreshore lease agreement.

**[00:31:19.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000522)  
So, Mr. Chair, before the ANR permits or recognizes use of a disputed shoreline,

**[00:31:25.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000523)  
has it conclusively determined the land's legal status?

**[00:31:29.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000524)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:31:31.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000525)  
Please go ahead.

**[00:31:33.000] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000526)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:31:34.060] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000527)  
For these foreshore leases,

**[00:31:37.040] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000528)  
meron po kaming mga maps,

**[00:31:40.200] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000529)  
the maps that are in custody of the department.

**[00:31:43.520] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000530)  
And we are currently updating the foreshore

**[00:31:48.720] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000531)  
kasi kung may marami ng pagbabago sa foreshore area.

**[00:31:52.380] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000532)  
For example, the cadastral survey has been conducted many years ago.

**[00:31:57.220] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000533)  
And so we now have the foreshore delineation and profiling activity

**[00:32:01.160] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000534)  
that is funded, Mr. and Your Honor.

**[00:32:04.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000535)  
Sige.

**[00:32:05.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000536)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:32:06.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000537)  
Kung meron pong mga fisher folks na nag-o-occupy ng disposable foreshore public land,

**[00:32:12.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000538)  
ano po yung mga tenure option na available para sa kanila?

**[00:32:19.040] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000539)  
For fisher folks, sir, Your Honor,

**[00:32:22.620] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000540)  
through that activity of foreshore delineation,

**[00:32:26.460] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000541)  
we are able to determine kung saan ba talaga naka-okupa ang mga fisher folks.

**[00:32:32.440] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000542)  
So, for example, they are within the A&D.

**[00:32:36.720] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000543)  
For example,

**[00:32:37.720] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000544)  
kasi minsan akala nila nasa foreshore sila but nasa A&D naman pala.

**[00:32:42.560] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000545)  
For example, sir, when we piloted the foreshore delineation in Region 1,

**[00:32:47.520] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000546)  
it was found out that meron kaming specific fisher folks doon na tinignan

**[00:32:54.020] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000547)  
and we found out that nasa A&D sila.

**[00:32:56.860] LMB Director Emelyne V. Talabis** (asr-000548)  
So, sa ngayon, pinoprocess na po yung mga applications nila.

**[00:33:00.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000549)  
Okay po.

**[00:33:00.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000550)  
Mr. Chair, dahil 38 seconds na lang po ako,

**[00:33:03.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000551)  
Hindi, sandali lang, sandali lang.

**[00:33:05.600] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000552)  
I-stop ko yung clock.

**[00:33:06.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000553)  
Okay po.

**[00:33:08.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000554)  
Pag sinabi mong foreshore lease, doon yan sa dagat na talaga, hindi sa lupa.

**[00:33:14.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000555)  
Di ba?

**[00:33:15.240] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000556)  
Now, kung yung mga fisher folks, in addition to doon sa explanation,

**[00:33:20.400] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000557)  
maibigay ko lang din for the information of our colleagues and everyone here,

**[00:33:25.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000558)  
Yung Philippine Reclamation Authority, basta meron pong puwedeng i-reclaim, maski konti.

**[00:33:31.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000559)  
Lalo na maraming maraming fisher folks doon.

**[00:33:34.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000560)  
All you have to do is write to the PRA,

**[00:33:37.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000561)  
ang gawin mong land for the landless.

**[00:33:39.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000562)  
So, meron silang programang ganun.

**[00:33:41.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000563)  
So, hindi na kailangan sila ng foreshore lease.

**[00:33:44.640] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000564)  
Kasi pag foreshore lease, hindi natin siguradong malalim ba yun.

**[00:33:48.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000565)  
O, ang ina-applyan ng foreshore lease, yung mababaw lang eh.

**[00:33:52.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000566)  
Yung malalim, hindi pwede yun.

**[00:33:53.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000567)  
Di ba?

**[00:33:54.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000568)  
So, ganun yun, dear colleague, na kung marami ang fisher folks,

**[00:33:59.920] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000569)  
makakatulong ako sa inyo dahil galing ako sa PRA

**[00:34:02.500] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000570)  
and I share this information

**[00:34:04.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000571)  
that kung matambakan mo yung merong

**[00:34:07.100] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000572)  
a reef or bahura

**[00:34:08.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000573)  
and you can apply land for the landless,

**[00:34:11.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000574)  
ibigay nila yun, permit sa iyo.

**[00:34:12.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000575)  
Hindi ka pa pahirapan nun.

**[00:34:14.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000576)  
And I will go with you kung kailangan na kailangan yun.

**[00:34:16.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000577)  
Thank you.

**[00:34:17.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000578)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:34:19.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000579)  
Since limited po ang time ko,

**[00:34:20.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000580)  
meron po akong mga hihinging dokumento na lang po sa DENR

**[00:34:23.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000581)  
regarding po sa mga bagay na to.

**[00:34:25.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000582)  
And, Mr. Chair, I am signifying my intent for second round.

**[00:34:29.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000583)  
Yun lang po, Mr. Chair. Maraming salamat po.

**[00:34:31.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000584)  
Okay, maraming salamat.

**[00:34:33.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000585)  
We go to the next.

**[00:34:35.200] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000586)  
Dito pa siya, ano?

**[00:34:37.340] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000587)  
Rep. Adrian. Adrian Amatong.

**[00:34:39.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000588)  
Dito na.

**[00:34:40.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000589)  
Ayp.

**[00:34:41.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000590)

**[00:34:45.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000593)  
Hi.

**[00:34:46.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000594)  
Adrian.

**[00:34:47.160] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000595)  
Rep. Adrian.

**[00:34:48.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000596)  
Go ahead. You have the floor, sir.

**[00:34:50.740] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000597)

**[00:34:52.160] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000598)

**[00:34:54.300] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000599)

**[00:34:59.060] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000600)  
Medyo naratakot ako kasi

**[00:35:01.040] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000601)  
ang intention nila yata nila eh

**[00:35:52.040] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000622)  
That company started wrong.

**[00:35:55.000] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000624)

**[00:36:03.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000628)

**[00:36:11.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000631)

**[00:36:14.040] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000633)

**[00:36:16.900] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000634)

**[00:36:19.700] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000635)

**[00:36:28.820] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000639)

**[00:36:32.620] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000640)

**[00:36:33.940] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000641)

**[00:36:36.440] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000642)

**[00:36:39.440] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000643)

**[00:36:41.100] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000644)

**[00:36:43.280] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000645)  
Department of Environmental,

**[00:36:44.220] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000646)

**[00:36:45.340] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000647)

**[00:36:48.100] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000648)  
So, yun lang po.

**[00:36:49.040] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000649)  
Kasi natatakot ako,

**[00:36:50.000] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000650)  
pati yung mga constituents ko,

**[00:36:51.300] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000651)

**[00:36:53.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000652)  
doon sa area namin.

Approximately 53.6 million, Mr. Chair.

**[00:52:02.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001015)  
53.6 billion po. Paano naman po nasisiguro ng DNR na ang pondo ay ginagamit para protektahan ang karapatan ng mga communities at LGUs na dapat konsultahin bago ang renewal o extension ng mga kasunduan?

**[00:52:23.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001016)  
May answer?

**[00:52:24.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001017)  
May I defer to MGB? Director Larry Heradez, please.

**[00:52:28.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001018)  
Director, please respond.

**[00:52:35.460] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001019)  
Meron po tayong mga programa ngayon sa DENR-MGB na tulungan yung mga small-scale miners, including po yung mga unregistered small-scale miners.

**[00:52:47.400] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001020)  
Ang isang proyekto po na inumpisan po ng DNR, yung pong pagbibigay po ng interim small-scale miners.

**[00:52:56.940] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001021)  
At

**[00:52:58.200] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001022)  
hikayatin yung mga illegal miners to register and as well as to capacitate and train them to the proper ways of mining and processing of mineral ore.

**[00:53:09.420] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001023)  
Yun po yung ating ginagawa ngayon. Aside from the usual monitoring po ng small and large-scale mining operations po sa buong bansa po.

**[00:53:19.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001024)  
Thank you. Representative?

**[00:53:21.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001025)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:53:22.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001026)  
Kaugnay po ng naunang katanungan ko.

**[00:53:25.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001027)  
Gusto ko lang po i-raise ang mga kongkretong kaso.

**[00:53:28.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001028)  
Patungkol sa FTAA.

**[00:53:29.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001029)  
Nabanggit po kasi kanina na tinutulungan yung mga small-scale miners.

**[00:53:34.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001030)  
Pero paano naman po yung mga community na merong kaso o merong problema?

**[00:53:39.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001031)  
Sa Didipio, Nueva Vizcaya, nirenew ng gobyerno ang FTAA No. 001 kahit walang consultation at prior consent mula sa communities at LGUs.

**[00:53:52.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001032)  
Gaya ng requirements sa local government code at EIS system.

**[00:53:57.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001033)  
Bakit po ito napayagan ng DNR?

**[00:54:00.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001034)  
Pwede po bang i-explain ito dito sa committee?

**[00:54:04.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001035)  
Why this is not a violation of your mandate?

**[00:54:07.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001036)  
Secretary?

**[00:54:10.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001037)  
Director, this will answer that.

**[00:54:14.420] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001038)  
Mr. Chairman, yung pong tinutukoy ng FTAA yung pong sa Oceana Gold po kung di po ako nagkakamali.

**[00:54:22.940] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001039)  
Yung pong Oceana Gold when it was...

**[00:54:25.720] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001040)  
nag-expired na po siya, yung pong renewal niya ay umakyat pa po sa Office of the President.

**[00:54:33.820] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001041)  
And it has gone through various process of securing po yung particularly yung FPIC including yung social acceptability requirement.

**[00:54:46.240] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001042)  
Kung meron po ngayon mga statement na the company is operating without public acceptability,

**[00:54:56.560] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001043)  
ay handa po naman.

**[00:54:58.320] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001044)  
Ang MGB na imbestigahan po yung mga allegations or yung mga facts na yun kung ito po ay may basihan na wala talagang nakuha yung mining company na social acceptability requirement.

**[00:55:13.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001045)  
Pero yung pong sinasabi ko lang po, yung pong FTAA actually has gone the first term of its 25 years and is working, it is now operating under the second term.

**[00:55:25.400] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001046)  
And wala po kami ng mga...

**[00:55:28.200] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001047)  
na-receive na report na merong mga abuses na ginagawa ng mining company.

**[00:55:37.600] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-001048)  
But we will be ready to investigate po yung mga issues na non-acceptability ng community doon sa mining operation po.

**[00:55:46.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001049)  
Mr. Chair, ang malas ko siguro no dahil ako yung naka-receive ng report about this.

**[00:55:52.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001050)  
But ito na lang po ang hilingin ko sa DNR.

**[00:55:55.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001051)  
Pwede po ba yung DENR makapagbigay

**[00:55:58.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001052)  
sa atin ng data na nagpapatunay na kumpleto po sila ng requirements at hindi po sila lumabag doon or nagkaroon ng violation sa kanilang mandate.

**[00:56:09.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001053)  
Pangalawang kaso po, ganoon din po sa Tampakan, South Cotabato, kung saan nirenew at in-extend pa ang 18 years na FTAA No. 002 ng DENR at MGB,

**[00:56:23.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001054)  
muli ring walang consultation at prior consent mula sa communities at LGUs.

**[00:56:29.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001055)  
Ano po kaya ang accountability mechanisms ng DNR para sa ganitong mga renewal na lumalabag sa participatory requirements ng batas, especially sa IPRA?

**[00:56:41.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001056)  
Secretary, please.

**[00:56:45.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001057)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:56:46.860] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001058)  
Actually, itong meron pong consultation na dinadaanan po lahat yan, particularly kung meron na po silang existing na ECC, meron pong public consultation na dinadaanan yan.

**[00:56:59.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001059)  
And so, with the indulgence of the committee, Mr. Chair, we will submit all these documents to the Honorable Congresswoman after this hearing, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:11.900] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001060)  
Thank you. Please submit to Congresswoman Ismula.

**[00:57:15.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001061)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:16.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001062)  
So, we will have a submission regarding Tampakan. Thank you.

**[00:57:18.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001063)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:19.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001064)  
Just put a note na lang po, Mr. Chair, sa ating ginoong sekretary na kadalasan po kasi ang nangyayari kaya siya na-re-renew ng hindi na nagkakaroon ng konsultasyon dahil nagkaroon na siya ng unang approval.

**[00:57:34.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001065)  
At nung nag-expire po siya, hindi po nila ito kinonsulta ang community ulit.

**[00:57:39.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001066)  
Hindi po ba sa patakaran ng ating IPRA, kinakailangan po na magkonsulta po ulit kung ito ay nag-expire na.

**[00:57:48.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001067)  
Dadako naman po ako.

**[00:57:49.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001068)  
On waste management, ano ang hakbang ng DNR para masiguro na ang LGU waste management contractors ay dumadaan sa mahigpit na vetting process at hindi nagiging instrumento ng conflict of interest ng mga lokal na opisyal?

**[00:58:06.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001069)  
Meron po bang oversight ang DNR dito?

**[00:58:10.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001070)  
Secretary, who will answer that question?

**[00:58:13.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001071)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair, for the question.

**[00:58:19.200] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001072)  
As we know, under the law, RA

**[00:58:21.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001073)  
9003.

**[00:58:22.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001074)  
The local government units have the responsibility when it comes to the waste management within their areas of jurisdiction.

**[00:58:30.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001075)  
Ang responsibility naman po ng DNR is to provide technical assistance and to educate ang ating mga local government units, mga barangay,

**[00:58:42.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001076)  
on how we can assist in putting up the materials recovery facilities, in helping educate the people on segregation,

**[00:58:51.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001077)  
and other aspects of the law.

**[00:58:54.660] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001078)  
But pagdating po sa pag-engage ng private entity to do the waste management for the LGUs,

**[00:59:05.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001079)  
wala po kaming mandato to really look into the particular, alam po nila, Congressman Erice being also involved in the LGU.

**[00:59:16.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001080)  
So in that aspect, wala po kaming...

**[00:59:19.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001081)  
Opinion or comment on this, Mr. Chair?

**[00:59:24.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001082)  
Sakali po, Mr. Chair, na naintindihan ko po kung sinasabi po ng ating ginoong sekretary na wala silang mandato dito, no?

**[00:59:32.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001083)  
Pero sakali po kayang may nai-report po sa kanila na ganito at pinapa-imbestigahan sa kanila, may action po ba silang ginagawa?

**[00:59:41.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001084)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:42.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001085)  
Opo, meron naman po.

**[00:59:43.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001086)  
We call the attention of the local government unit at nakipag-ugnayan po kami.

**[00:59:48.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001087)  
Actually, with the local government units,

**[00:59:50.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001088)  
with Waste, tuloy-tuloy po ang coordination namin sa kanila, our regional offices,

**[00:59:55.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001089)  
continuously,

**[00:59:56.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001090)  
particularly the EMB, are in coordination with the local government units on solid waste management, Mr. Chair?

**[01:00:04.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001091)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:05.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001092)  
I have one more question regarding waste management.

**[01:00:09.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001093)  
If the waste management provider's contract is up for renewal,

**[01:00:15.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001094)  
will we have another consultation with the community regarding the performance of the contractor?

**[01:00:23.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001095)  
Secretary, yes.

**[01:00:26.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001096)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:27.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001097)  
Actually, that exercise is purely with the LGU, Mr. Chair, and we are not part of that process, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:36.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001098)  
Mr. Chair, it looks like you're teaching because when we consulted, the LGU itself told us that it's the DENR.

**[01:00:44.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001099)  
Anyway, I'm asking you...

**[01:00:46.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001100)  
Which one, Secretary?

**[01:00:46.940] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001101)  
The statement of the Honorable Smula is that there has to be one primary...

**[01:00:53.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001102)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:55.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001103)  
We are not involved

**[01:00:57.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001104)  
in the renewal of contracts of the private entities,

**[01:01:01.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001105)  
but it is within our mandate to monitor, for instance, sanitary landfills on compliance with the ECC conditions, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:13.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001106)  
Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:14.500] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001107)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[01:01:15.280] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001108)  
Yes, Mr. Congressman Egay.

**[01:01:17.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001109)  
There's local government autonomy.

**[01:01:19.240] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001110)  
So, the waste management providers are purely local.

**[01:01:23.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001111)  
If there's a complaint, just go to the DILG.

**[01:01:25.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001112)  
It's not DENR.

**[01:01:27.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001113)  
Thank you.

**[01:01:27.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001114)  
Maraming salamat ho, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:28.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001115)  
Thank you, Congressman Erice.

**[01:01:29.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001116)  
Last but not least, for waste management, what does the DENR do to ensure that waste pickers have the right compensation and protection according to the Occupational Safety and Health Standards?

**[01:01:45.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001117)  
And how do contractors monitor compliance?

**[01:01:50.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001118)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:51.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001119)  
If I may defer to Assistant Secretary Noreen Ran of Enforcement, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:55.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001120)  
Under Secretary Noreen Ran.

**[01:01:57.140] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001121)  
Please proceed.

**[01:01:58.150] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001122)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:00.060] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001123)  
What we're doing when it comes to this, in fact, with the RA 9003,

**[01:02:05.080] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001124)  
it's prohibited for the settlers in our sanitary landfills.

**[01:02:09.460] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001125)  
It's prohibited.

**[01:02:10.360] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001126)  
So, the employment of the employees in the area,

**[01:02:16.100] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001127)  
there's no DENR mandate.

**[01:02:19.700] DENR Assistant Secretary Norlito A. Eneran** (asr-001128)  
This is purely an operation of our provider and LGU.

**[01:02:26.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001129)  
Okay.

**[01:02:27.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001130)  
I'll go to the wider discussion of forest governance and community rights.

**[01:02:33.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001131)  
The DENR announced 38 trillion pesos forest investments from 2027 to 2052,

**[01:02:41.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001132)  
which is expected to provide work and livelihood to rural communities.

**[01:02:46.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001133)  
In addition, the DENR released Department Administrative Order No. 2026-34

**[01:02:55.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001137)  
Distribution of the Forest Management Bureau

**[01:02:58.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001138)  
of more than 1.18 million hectares of potential investment areas

**[01:03:05.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001140)  
through the Sustainable Forest Land Management Agreements.

**[01:03:11.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001141)  
With such a large investment,

**[01:03:14.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001142)  
I would like to know how much was allocated to monitoring SFMAs and forest investment portfolio packages in NEP 2027.

**[01:03:26.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001144)  
Thank you very much Mr. Chair. I'd like to refer to the Forest Mansion Bureau Director Art Fadikella, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:37.880] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001145)  
Good afternoon, Mr. President. Good afternoon, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:03:43.540] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001146)  
Regarding the announcement of our distinguished congressman in our investment area,

**[01:03:51.940] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001147)  
it is true that we are dealing with 1.18 million hectares of potential investment area.

**[01:04:08.380] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001148)  
We are offering this to investors and once we have the applications and we have provided the tenured instruments,

**[01:04:21.940] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001149)  
we are ready to ensure that they follow the regulations and the conditions provided by the DNR.

**[01:04:35.660] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001150)  
Now, we have been given three Sustainable Forest Management Agreements

**[01:04:48.840] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001151)  
according to the Department of Administrative Order that has been issued.

**[01:04:52.880] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001152)  
And when they implement the projects,

**[01:05:02.400] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001153)  
we will give them to the Department of Administrative Order, to our regional offices for monitoring of what they are doing.

**[01:05:14.950] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-001154)  
So, we will give the funds when the projects are ready.

**[01:05:25.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001155)  
Mr. Chair, thank you first to FMB Director Art Fadikella.

**[01:05:32.650] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001156)  
Please proceed. Yes, we'll give you more time.

**[01:05:34.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001157)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, this is the last time.

**[01:05:35.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001159)  
It looks like I didn't get the answer that I needed.

**[01:05:38.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001160)  
What I am asking is, if we have this kind of program,

**[01:05:45.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001161)  
what I just want to see is how much we have invested in NEP for 2027.

**[01:05:50.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001162)  
Budget, how much is placed there?

**[01:05:53.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001163)  
Mr. Chair, if I may defer to Usec Jonas Leones, who supervises the Forest Management Bureau.

**[01:06:00.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001164)  
Usec Leones.

**[01:06:01.500] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001165)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:06:03.040] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001166)  
Just to respond

**[01:06:04.360] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001167)  
to the question of the Honorable Congresswoman,

**[01:06:07.400] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001168)  
since the program is still new,

**[01:06:12.320] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001169)

**[01:06:18.680] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001170)

**[01:06:22.060] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001171)  
that we have already allocated for 2027 an amount of 60 million

**[01:06:28.420] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001172)

**[01:06:34.860] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001173)  
Madam Congresswoman.

**[01:06:36.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001174)

**[01:06:37.460] DENR Undersecretary Jonas R. Leones** (asr-001175)  
60 million.

**[01:06:38.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001176)  
Yes.

**[01:06:39.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001177)  
That's the answer, Representative Ismula.

**[01:06:41.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001178)  
Yes.

**[01:06:41.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001179)

Thank you very much to our DNR.

Thank you very much.

**[01:07:09.900] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001191)

**[01:07:33.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001199)

**[01:07:59.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001210)

**[01:08:16.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001214)

**[01:08:19.240] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001215)

**[01:08:33.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001217)  
we have here Bong Teves, Jr.

**[01:08:36.780] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001218)
