**[01:08:41.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001221)  
Additional pa? Meron pa?

**[01:08:43.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001223)  
Ah, omnibus talaga ito.

**[01:08:44.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001224)  
Meron pa dumating.

**[01:08:45.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001225)  
Importante talaga ang DENR to Congress.

**[01:08:49.380] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001226)  
Assistant Minority Leader Sarah Jane Elago is now with us.

**[01:08:53.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001227)  
Deputy Majority Leader, Vice Chair, of course, Tevez has already been recognized.

**[01:08:59.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001228)  
Representative Maximo Y. Dalog, Jr.

**[01:09:02.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001229)  
Representative Ed Lumayag.

**[01:09:05.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001230)  
Representative Munir Arbison.

**[01:09:08.020] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001231)  
Then my cousin, Representative Leo Rodriguez, the more handsome one.

**[01:09:13.780] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001232)  
Assistant Majority Leader Antonio "Tonio" Roman III.

**[01:09:18.500] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001233)  
Thank you.

**[01:09:19.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001234)  
Let us now proceed with the interpellation.

**[01:09:21.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001235)  
So we are now back to the minority.

**[01:09:25.240] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001236)  
Majority.

**[01:09:26.420] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001238)  
And we have here Congressman, our Deputy Speaker before.

**[01:09:31.940] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001239)  
Bong Tevez, the floor is yours.

**[01:09:35.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001240)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:09:38.500] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001241)  
Fellow Congressmen, members of the DNR, headed by Secretary Attorney Juan Miguel Cuna.

**[01:09:47.225] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001242)  
Magandang tanghali sa inyo lahat.

**[01:09:52.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001243)  
Before I ask question, meron ba rito ng representative from National Solid Waste Management Commission?

**[01:09:59.960] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001244)  
Is there a presence?

**[01:10:02.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001245)  
Well, Mr. Chair.

**[01:10:03.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001246)  
The DNR chairs that commission and the representative Undersecretary Rodriguez is here, Mr. Chair.

**[01:10:13.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001247)  
Another cousin.

**[01:10:14.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001248)  
Where is Mr. Rodriguez?

**[01:10:18.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001249)  
Over there.

**[01:10:21.320] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001250)  
Please reply to the question.

**[01:10:23.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001251)  
Do you have a question already?

**[01:10:25.250] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001252)  
Not yet.

**[01:10:25.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001253)  
So we'll propound the question now.

**[01:10:27.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001254)  
Okay, thank you.

**[01:10:28.448] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001255)  
Mr. Chair.

**[01:10:31.755] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001256)  
Lagi pong sinasabi.

**[01:10:33.753] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001257)  
Every budget hearing that the National Solid Waste Management Commission is a separate office under the Office of the President.

**[01:10:44.741] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001258)  
However, Mr. Chair, for the information of everybody, the Chairman of the Commission is the Secretary of the DNR.

**[01:10:55.730] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001259)  
Kaya po malaki ang ginagampanan ng DNR sa komisyong ito.

**[01:11:01.724] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001260)  
Ang Environmental Management Bureau din po ng DNR ay may mandato na mag-monitor at mag-imporse ng environmental law kasama ang Republic Act 9003.

**[01:11:17.707] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001261)  
Mr. Chair, last budget hearing.

**[01:11:23.621] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001262)  
Ang ating po nga sekretary ngayon ay dati pong Undersecretary in charge of Solid Waste Management.

**[01:11:34.649] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001263)  
That is why alam po niya ang problema dahil palagi ko tinatanong sa kanya regarding Republic Act 9003.

**[01:11:45.638] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001264)  
Ang sabi niya po sa akin nung mga nakarang taon kanya na pong inaayos ang mga problema

**[01:11:57.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001265)  
sa mga waste management para hindi na magkaroon ng problema sa public health loan.

**[01:12:06.440] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001268)  
Mr. Chair, I was there a while ago for the deliberation of the DPWH.

**[01:12:16.806] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001269)  
What is the topic of discussion there?

**[01:12:19.822] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001270)  
The problem of flooding.

**[01:12:24.298] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001271)  
Now, I am here for the deliberation of the DNR.

**[01:12:28.933] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001272)  
And last week, I gave a privileged speech and I said myself that the National Solid Waste Commission is inutile.

**[01:12:39.242] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001273)  
Because this law has been around for 25 years, we are always in Grade 2.

**[01:12:46.615] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001274)  
It means that the flooding problem in our society in Metro Manila is not only the problem of the DPWH,

**[01:12:57.963] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001275)  
but also the problem of the DPWH.

**[01:12:58.902] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001276)  
The problem is also the garbage that is being washed in the canals.

**[01:13:08.442] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001277)  
Now, Mr. Chair, I would like to ask Secretary Cunha,

**[01:13:13.737] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001278)  
what are your concrete plans to solve this garbage crisis which floods our nation every time a typhoon hits our country?

**[01:13:24.745] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001279)  
As a Commission Chairman of the National Solid Waste Commission,

**[01:13:30.379] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001280)  
what are your concrete plans to solve this garbage crisis which floods our nation every time a typhoon hits our country?

**[01:13:31.678] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001281)  
Secretary, please answer another privileged speech given by Congressman Bong Temes.

**[01:13:40.389] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001282)  
Kindly answer.

**[01:13:42.087] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001283)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:13:43.745] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001284)  
Actually, solid waste management has been a big issue for the last few weeks,

**[01:13:51.158] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001285)  
particularly because of the heavy rains that we have been experiencing throughout the country,

**[01:13:55.913] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001286)  
and particularly here in the National Capital Region, Mr. Chair.

**[01:14:00.348] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001287)  
In fact, just to share with the body,

**[01:14:04.424] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001288)  
the Executive Secretary called for a meeting last week,

**[01:14:07.041] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001289)  
which I participated in along with MMDA and our other agencies, including DPWH,

**[01:14:15.672] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001290)  
which talked about the problem of garbage,

**[01:14:20.088] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001291)  
which is a big contributor in terms of the heavy flooding that we have been experiencing lately.

**[01:14:30.317] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001292)  
The Commission, Mr. Chair, that you're referring to,

**[01:14:34.393] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001293)  
of course, it's a policy.

**[01:14:36.411] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001294)  
It was also discussed in the Commission that maybe one of the things that we really need to pursue is our enforcement.

**[01:14:49.098] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001295)  
Because we are always asked when...

**[01:14:56.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001296)  
pagka meron mga proposals even way back more than 10 years ago,

**[01:15:02.933] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001297)  
nung nag-uumpisa pa lamang mag-introduce sa mga LGUs ng ordinances that would ban single-use plastics,

**[01:15:11.544] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001298)  
kung anong opinion po natin in the DNR.

**[01:15:14.361] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001299)  
And yung sinasagot po natin is that,

**[01:15:18.097] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001300)  
siyempre we support anything that would lessen any type of solid waste in circulation,

**[01:15:24.990] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001301)  
pero palagay ko po ang mahalaga ay yung tamang pagtatapon lang po.

**[01:15:30.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001302)  
So the proper waste disposal is the root cause of the contribution of solid waste dun sa flooding po natin.

**[01:15:40.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001303)  
So enforcement, in fact, napag-usapan po namin nila SILG at MMDA last week

**[01:15:46.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001304)  
when the President was called for a briefing on the situation dito sa Metro Manila

**[01:15:53.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001305)  
regarding solid waste disposal.

**[01:15:55.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001306)  
At I think it's high time na magkaroon po tayo ng honest-to-goodness enforcement also,

**[01:16:03.530] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001307)  
not just the LGUs, pati po kami at DILG,

**[01:16:08.665] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001308)  
to ensure that the different local government units, particularly the barangay,

**[01:16:13.680] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001309)  
take care of their areas of jurisdiction and ensure that proper solid waste management is observed.

**[01:16:21.072] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001310)  
Dahil ito pong nagiging problema talaga natin kung,

**[01:16:24.928] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001311)  
at ano pang material po ang pinag-uusapan natin, whether it's plastics, whether it's anything else,

**[01:16:30.303] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001312)  
if it's properly disposed of, we would not be experiencing what we are experiencing now, Mr. Chair.

**[01:16:35.577] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001313)  
So, to your point, Mr. Chair, maybe the Commission needs to come up with better, more concrete policies

**[01:16:44.728] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001314)  
to ensure enforcement, the proper use of the materials recovery facilities,

**[01:16:51.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001315)  
which are mandated by the law.

**[01:16:54.897] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001316)  
Which, admittedly, hindi pa po fully compliant lahat ng 42,000 barangays.

**[01:17:01.810] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001317)  
Meron pa rin po pagkukulang, or meron pong mga nakatayong mga MRF in certain LGUs,

**[01:17:08.583] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001318)  
but which are not actually properly used.

**[01:17:11.740] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001319)  
Para lang for compliance sake, meron po silang nakatayong MRF.

**[01:17:15.835] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001320)  
And, of course, napapag-usapan lately po ang possible use of technology,

**[01:17:24.866] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001321)  
to address yung solid waste management problem po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:17:28.662] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001322)  
Congressman Teves?

**[01:17:30.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001323)  
Mr. Chair, based sa inyong NIP, mayroong 92% compliance ng mga LGUs sa Ecological Solid Waste Management Act.

**[01:17:42.707] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001324)  
At may target kayong gawing 95% ito sa year 2027.

**[01:17:48.202] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001325)  
Ibig sabihin, kung titingnan natin, is almost 100% neto.

**[01:17:53.676] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001326)  
92% neto.

**[01:17:54.835] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001327)  
Pero ang tanong, bakit sobra pa rin ang baha?

**[01:17:58.831] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001328)  
And then, based sa Solid Waste Management Monitoring of the MMDA,

**[01:18:06.083] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001329)  
only 6 out of 17 LGUs in Metro Manila are compliant with the segregation at source mandated by Republic Act 9003.

**[01:18:20.288] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001330)  
What is the DENR doing to ensure that the others...

**[01:18:24.804] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001331)  
11 LGUs will be compliant?

**[01:18:27.921] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001332)  
Before the Secretary answers, let me just acknowledge the House of Representatives to the Commission on Appointments,

**[01:18:36.272] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001333)  
the Honorable Tony Pett Albano.

**[01:18:38.669] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001334)  
Please reply now.

**[01:18:42.825] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001335)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[01:18:47.141] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001336)  
Before I defer to the Environmental Management Bureau,

**[01:18:49.658] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001337)  
I think, if I'm not mistaken, that 92% compliance,

**[01:18:56.371] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001338)  
was pertaining to submission of Solid Waste Management Plans, Mr. Chair.

**[01:19:01.306] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001339)  
So, meron pong provision under RA 9003, which requires all the local government units to submit 10-year Solid Waste Management Plans.

**[01:19:15.012] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001340)  
So, the law was signed way back in 2001.

**[01:19:21.185] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001341)  
So, ngayon po, 92% na po ang nakapag-submit ng Solid Waste Management Plans.

**[01:19:26.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001342)  
But the challenge is the implementation of these plans that have been submitted.

**[01:19:31.994] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001343)  
I'd like to defer, Mr. Chair, with the kind indulgence of the Committee to Atty. Matias of the EMB to provide more information, Mr. Chair.

**[01:19:43.242] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001344)  
Atty. Matias is recognized.

**[01:19:45.860] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001345)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:19:47.638] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001346)  
For Solid Waste Management, sir, tama po na.

**[01:19:51.953] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001347)  
It's 92%, but as said by the Secretary, the Honorable Secretary,

**[01:19:56.309] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001348)  
it's only insofar as the approval of the plans.

**[01:20:00.085] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001349)  
Na-approve po ito ng Solid Waste Management Commission,

**[01:20:02.842] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001350)  
and ang focus po natin right now would be to monitor their compliance insofar as the plan approved by the Solid Waste Management Commission.

**[01:20:12.552] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001351)  
And supposed to be po, I mean, the plan should include, sir, yung segregation at source, segregated collection,

**[01:20:20.783] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001352)  
and as mandated po by law,

**[01:20:23.920] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001353)  
ang dapat pong napupunta sa ating,

**[01:20:26.278] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001354)  
yung mga sanitary landfills ay residual waste lang po.

**[01:20:29.375] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001355)  
Kaya po, kung mapapansin po natin,

**[01:20:31.652] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001356)  
mixed waste po yung mga nasa sanitary landfills,

**[01:20:34.669] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001357)  
kaya medyo mabilis po siyang mapuno,

**[01:20:36.967] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001358)  
and nakakaroon po ng problema, kinalaunan.

**[01:20:40.123] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001359)  
That's why, as part of the instruction to us by the Secretary,

**[01:20:45.758] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001360)  
we will now also be focusing on compliance by establishments,

**[01:20:50.453] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001361)  
pagdating po sa segregation at source,

**[01:20:51.780] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001362)  
and at the same time, we will likewise be monitoring yung segregation,

**[01:20:56.380] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001365)  
leading to the sanitary landfills po,

**[01:20:59.943] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001366)  
to make sure that yung mapupunta lang po sa sanitary landfills ay mga residual waste.

**[01:21:06.656] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001367)  
But aminado po tayo that we will have to work with different stakeholders po,

**[01:21:11.391] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001368)  
and other government agencies like the DILG,

**[01:21:14.288] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001369)  
to achieve po yung objective natin of segregated collection po.

**[01:21:20.132] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001370)  
Mr. Chair?

**[01:21:21.011] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001371)  
Please proceed, Congressman Tevez.

**[01:21:24.088] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001372)  
Ito ba ay paper compliance?

**[01:21:26.865] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001373)  
Paano nyo ito namamunin?

**[01:21:28.164] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001374)  
Please answer, Attorney Matias.

**[01:21:32.539] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001375)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:21:33.138] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001376)  
The department issued an instruction to the regional offices

**[01:21:37.514] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001377)  
requiring the targets of compliance ng mga establishments po natin

**[01:21:43.807] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001378)  
insofar as segregation at source,

**[01:21:46.205] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001379)  
and likewise look into the segregated collection.

**[01:21:51.200] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001380)  
Pagdating po dito sa mga waste,

**[01:21:53.038] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001381)  
kasi ang experience po natin,

**[01:21:55.016] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001382)  
although some establishments are practicing segregation,

**[01:21:58.132] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001383)  
segregation at source,

**[01:21:59.631] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001384)  
pero yung collection po, magkakasama pa rin po.

**[01:22:02.288] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001385)  
And ito po yung binabantayan po natin.

**[01:22:05.405] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-001386)  
And corresponding notices will be issued to those involved in the activity, sir.

**[01:22:13.596] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001387)  
Thank you. Congressman Tevez?

**[01:22:15.754] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001388)  
Mr. Chair, may partner ba kayo sa sinasabing yung segregation at source?

**[01:22:21.069] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001389)  
Kasi tinuturoan nyo na mag-segregate ang household basis sa

**[01:22:28.101] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001390)  
sa Republic Act 9003.

**[01:22:31.358] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001391)  
But ang tanong ko, may partner ba kayo para humakot sa inyo ng recyclables?

**[01:22:39.470] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001392)  
Siguro pwedeng sumagot sa akin ng taga National Solid Waste Commission o taga ENB.

**[01:22:44.784] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001393)  
Kasi wala akong nakikitang partners nyo.

**[01:22:49.729] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001394)  
Anong nangyayari, Mr. Chair?

**[01:22:51.787] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001395)  
Turo sila ng turo kung paano mag-segregate.

**[01:22:54.744] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001396)  
After nilang maturo, wala silang ipakikilala kung sino ang hahakot ng recyclables.

**[01:23:05.075] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-001399)  
Thank you. You have 35 seconds more.

**[01:23:08.028] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001400)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. I'd like to defer to Usec. Rodriguez who chairs the commission.

**[01:23:13.411] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001401)  
Good morning, po.

**[01:23:15.600] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001402)  
Sir, I'd like to answer first your previous question regarding the National Solid Waste Management Commission.

**[01:23:21.120] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001403)  
So it is correct that it actually is under the office of the President.

**[01:23:24.840] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001404)  
And it is chaired by the DENR.

**[01:23:28.080] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001405)  
But the way that the commission...

**[01:23:31.740] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001406)  
The commission was set up, actually, even 25 years ago,

**[01:23:35.240] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001407)  
yung members po niya, whole of society approached na.

**[01:23:38.480] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001408)  
We have various government agencies represented.

**[01:23:42.760] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001409)  
Meron pong tatlong NGO kasama po doon ng eco-waste.

**[01:23:46.160] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001410)  
At kasama po ang private sector.

**[01:23:48.020] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001411)  
Nakasama pong mga nasa packaging at mga ibang-ibang mga ano, no?

**[01:23:52.380] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001412)  
Na nag-generate po ng solid waste.

**[01:23:53.820] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001413)  
Ang budget po ng National Solid Waste Management Commission is zero.

**[01:23:59.540] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001414)  
Okay, wala po siyang budget.

**[01:24:01.120] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001415)  
Nakukuha lang po siya sa EMB.

**[01:24:02.920] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001416)  
Under 9003, EMB will act as a secretariat.

**[01:24:07.460] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001417)  
Ang nakukuha lang pong budget ng National Solid Waste Commission from the EMB is only around 3.5 million pesos a year.

**[01:24:15.880] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001418)  
So, ngayon po, wala pong staffing ang commission.

**[01:24:18.540] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001419)  
At the same time, ang nangyayari po, no?

**[01:24:23.500] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001420)  
I think it was mentioned that out of the 1,592 LGUs who are supposed to submit their 10-year solid waste management,

**[01:24:32.180] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001421)  
only 92% is 1,460.

**[01:24:37.220] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001422)  
Ang ginagawa po ng commission is sila po dapat ang nag-check kung sumusunod po ang mga LGU sa mga kanilang mga plano.

**[01:24:48.140] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001423)  
Pero since wala pong tao ang commission, ang mga commissioners po mismo ang umiikot.

**[01:24:52.960] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001424)  
At ang naiikutan lang po dila, if I'm not mistaken, every year is around 12 to 20.

**[01:24:58.280] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001425)  
So, I think that also is one of the reasons.

**[01:25:00.000] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001426)  
Now, to answer.

**[01:25:01.560] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001427)  
So, whether may partners ang commission, one of the members of the commission is actually the DILG.

**[01:25:10.920] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001428)  
And we recognize that kung meron pong segregation, it has to start from the barangay level.

**[01:25:17.780] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001429)  
So, we just recently issued a resolution stating that or recommending that the DILG already activate their barangay level solid waste management.

**[01:25:31.400] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001430)  
committees po.

**[01:25:33.220] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001431)  
Yun po.

**[01:25:33.560] DENR Undersecretary for Special Concerns and Legislative Affairs Ignatius A. Rodriguez** (asr-001432)  
Thank you, sir.

**[01:44:35.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001984)  
campaign, maybe the EMB should be

**[01:44:37.620] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001985)  
directed to further

**[01:44:39.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001986)  
not only do it on our own,

**[01:44:41.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001987)  
it has to be done with the

**[01:44:42.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001988)  
DILG, with

**[01:44:45.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001989)  
DepEd. I actually mentioned this to

**[01:44:47.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001990)  
si Exani already, na

**[01:44:49.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001991)  
kailangan natin i-step up because

**[01:44:50.660] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001992)  
alam niyo po,

**[01:44:52.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001993)  
masakit man aminin na

**[01:44:54.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001994)  
na marami po tayong mga kababayan, talagang naging normal na sa kanila magtapon kahit saan.

**[01:45:03.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001995)  
Another problem which Secretary Aliling mentioned in the meeting we had with EES last week on flooding

**[01:45:10.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001996)  
is yung mga tao nakatira along the waterways is another concern that has to be addressed.

**[01:45:19.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001997)  
Dahil pag hindi po efficient yung collection, lalo na sa mga highly urbanized areas,

**[01:45:24.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001998)  
pagdating sa mga looban, kahit mali po siya, the most convenient thing would be to throw it in the waterways

**[01:45:33.240] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-001999)  
and let it go with the flow ng tubig po. So that's another issue po.

**[01:45:38.180] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002000)  
Thank you, Secretary. You mentioned that the law was passed in 2008, correct?

**[01:45:43.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002001)  
If my recollection serves me right.

**[01:45:46.020] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002002)  
Yes, because 2008.

**[01:45:48.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002003)  
Was you became the PENRO, right?

**[01:45:53.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002004)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:45:54.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002005)  
And then you became Regional Director of the NAR?

**[01:45:56.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002006)  
No, not Regional Director, Mr. Chair. Director.

**[01:45:58.900] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002007)  
So Assistant Secretary?

**[01:46:00.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002008)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:46:00.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002009)  
And then Undersecretary?

**[01:46:02.500] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002010)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:46:03.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002011)  
And now you are the Secretary who is a career official who always works with integrity and efficiency in our government.

**[01:46:13.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002012)  
Is that correct?

**[01:46:15.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002013)  
Let's give a big hand for the Secretary. Yan ang karir eh.

**[01:46:18.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002014)  
So therefore, we congratulate you and that is why we are having this budget hearing to increase your budget.

**[01:46:26.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002015)  
Thank you.

**[01:46:27.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002016)  
So, again, Col. Nica, please proceed.

**[01:46:30.960] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002017)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:46:33.220] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002018)  
So, last question. I think that was already brought up with my R.E.D. a while ago.

**[01:46:38.680] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002019)  
But I would just like to reiterate the status or, you know, to fund po sana yung reestablishment of political boundaries.

**[01:46:48.220] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002020)  
And control points and relocation of lots of Lahar-affected areas, specifically in Bacolor.

**[01:46:53.920] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002021)  
Because I came from Bacolor and we all know the history of Bacolor, no?

**[01:46:58.660] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002022)  
So, nalahar po yan. So, lahat ng tao na rin na-resettle na po yan sa ibang towns.

**[01:47:05.020] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002023)  
And, of course, since obligasyon po natin na hopefully makabalik sila, eh ang problema naman po kasi yung boundaries, no?

**[01:47:16.360] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002024)  
So, yung iba...

**[01:47:18.420] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002025)  
Asa na ba yung lote ko? They don't even know where their lot is.

**[01:47:22.000] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002026)  
So, I would just like to reiterate na sana po supportan po ng DNR yung aking request, yung funding po sa political boundaries and control points sa ating mga Lahar-affected areas.

**[01:47:34.160] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002027)  
So, that's all, Mr. Chair. And thank you, DNR family.

**[01:47:38.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002028)  
Thank you, Rep. Gonzalez. Do we get the commitment for additional funds for the district of Congressman Micla Gonzalez?

**[01:47:46.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002029)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Chair. Actually, we've spoken already.

**[01:47:48.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002030)  
Thank you to the Regional Executive Director, Mr. Chair, to submit this request, Mr. Chair.

**[01:47:54.700] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002031)  
Thank you. That's a very correct answer.

**[01:47:57.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002032)  
So, are you satisfied with that correct answer?

**[01:48:00.580] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-002033)  
I'm a very happy Congresswoman.

**[01:48:03.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002034)  
Thank you, Rep. Micla.

**[01:48:05.260] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002035)  
May I now call, now we go back to the minority.

**[01:48:08.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002036)  
And hear my good friend, who was with me in Germany and who is the representative of the island paradise of Dinagat Island.

**[01:48:21.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002038)  
Congresswoman Governor Kaka Bag-ao.

**[01:48:23.840] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002039)  
Good morning, Mr. Chair. Good morning po, Sec.

**[01:48:28.200] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002040)  
Law school pa lang. Sinalan na kita.

**[01:48:33.100] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002041)  
Ang tanong ko lang po, simple lang naman po talaga. Ang sadya ko ngayon.

**[01:48:37.160] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002042)  
Doon po sa budget ng monitoring ng DENR, lalo na ng MGB sa operation ng mga mining companies,

**[01:48:48.380] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002043)  
kasama halimbawa po ang magsasara na ng mining companies, kung sila ba ay compliant,

**[01:48:53.940] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002044)  
o kayo yung mga mining companies na may pending application, pero hindi pa napoprocess.

**[01:49:00.140] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002045)  
Magkano po yung budget ng MGB dyan?

**[01:49:03.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002046)  
And siguro, idugtong yun ah, in particular, alam ko, satellite office lang yung Dinagat.

**[01:49:08.400] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002047)  
So, magkano po yung sa Caraga?

**[01:49:12.060] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002048)  
Dalawang item lang po, national at regional.

**[01:49:16.040] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002049)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:18.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002050)  
I defer to Usec. Ana Teh of Finance.

**[01:49:20.240] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002051)  
Usec. Ana Teh, please respond.

**[01:49:22.820] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002052)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:24.060] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002053)  
The budget for monitoring under strengthening mineral regulation services through enhanced enforcement is about 53.6 million, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:34.140] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002054)  
53.6 million for the whole Philippines?

**[01:49:37.280] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002055)  
Million.

**[01:49:39.220] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002056)  
For the whole Philippines, ma'am?

**[01:49:41.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002057)  
Yes.

**[01:49:42.020] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002058)  
At sa karaga po?

**[01:49:44.380] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002059)  
How much for karaga?

**[01:49:45.700] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002060)  
Of the 63 million?

**[01:49:47.800] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002061)  
Million.

**[01:49:49.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002062)  
So, we await the figures to be given.

**[01:50:00.220] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002063)  
Can you proceed to the next question?

**[01:50:02.680] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002064)  
Yes, po.

**[01:50:03.320] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002066)  
I assume that you are getting the numbers.

**[01:50:08.100] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002067)  
However, ang gusto ko lang pong ipoint out that because an island where a lot of mining companies are present, like dinagat,

**[01:50:16.060] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002068)  
is considered as a satellite office, technically walang allocated na specific budget for monitoring.

**[01:50:23.320] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002069)  
So, I hope...

**[01:50:25.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002070)  
Hindi part ng monitoring ninyo na kapag nag-i-inform kayo sa probinsya kagaya ng dinagat ng mga status ng mga mining companies,

**[01:50:38.760] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002071)  
kung ito ay suspended, et cetera, ay hindi nyo idadaan sa Facebook.

**[01:50:42.240] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002072)  
In fact, yesterday, August 25, because there has already been clamor for closing a very small area in Hibusong Island,

**[01:50:56.330] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002073)  
What you did, ang ginawa po natin ay naglabas ng notice to the public, Sec.

**[01:51:00.760] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002074)  
At ang notice to the public ay dinaan sa Facebook.

**[01:51:05.680] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002075)  
At doon po sa notice, ang sabi po ninyo ay kahapon lang po ito,

**[01:51:09.940] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002076)  
hindi ko alam dahil ba ito ba dumudugtong sa budget hearing,

**[01:51:16.100] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002077)  
dahil may committee hearing tayo ngayon at may demand yung public to explain.

**[01:51:20.460] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002078)  
Ang Facebook page po ay contrary to what is circulating online.

**[01:51:25.760] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002079)  
There is no approved mineral exploration in Hibusong Island.

**[01:51:30.220] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002080)  
Pero alam po ng mga taga-Hibusong, taga-Dinagat, at ninyo din na may pwedeng application ang East Coast Mining Corporation.

**[01:51:37.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002081)  
At in fact, 10 years ago, sabi nyo, may suspension na ito noong 2014, suspension order noong 2014,

**[01:51:47.020] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002082)  
kung saan, kung resuman din po ako at naitanong ko na din po ito, 2014 pa po,

**[01:51:55.000] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002084)  
Ang threat ng operation ng East Coast Mining sa Dinagat Island, Sir,

**[01:52:00.420] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002085)  
I'm sure, please don't respond to me by saying that there is no mining operation in Hibusong.

**[01:52:06.600] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002086)  
Of course, everyday po nababantayan po yan ang mga tao.

**[01:52:10.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002087)  
At lagi pong may rally, kailang malungkot kapag ka-budget hearing,

**[01:52:14.320] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002088)  
itatanong namin ulit, ito pong suspension order, kailan po ito matatapos?

**[01:52:19.500] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002089)  
Kasi ang commitment po sa akin noong mga dati pong secretary pa,

**[01:52:24.100] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002090)  
Syempre mamalasing ka, sir, kasi maabutan ka, na ito po ay suspension order pa rin hanggang ngayon.

**[01:52:32.520] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002091)  
Kailan po ito talaga madedeclare na area closed for mining,

**[01:52:36.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002092)  
ilang beses na po din sa loob ng sampung taong ko sa Dinagat, bilang representative at governor din po,

**[01:52:43.500] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002093)  
ilang taon na din pong pinagdebatihan at naglabas na po ng study,

**[01:52:47.720] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002094)  
ang DENR po, that it should not be open for mining operations because of the vulnerability.

**[01:52:54.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002096)  
In fact, there are more than 100 areas that was already declared by LGUs at mga sangguniang barangay po,

**[01:53:06.360] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002097)  
declaring it conservation areas for protecting the vulnerability of the island.

**[01:53:14.640] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002098)  
So, ang tanong ko po, Sek, dahil wala po akong may tatanong sa budget,

**[01:53:19.680] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002099)  
dahil satellite po ang, alin po doon sa budget nyo, para hindi po po pipilitan na Facebook,

**[01:53:25.100] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002100)  
page lang ang communication.

**[01:53:26.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002101)  
In fact, sir, itong suspension order nyo, wala pong notice at wala pong communication sa LGU at sa mga tagat mamamayan ng Dinagat, sir.

**[01:53:38.760] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002102)  
Magpapasalamat lang ako kay Kong Dago, kasi kasama ko po siya nagpapainay sa issue na ito.

**[01:53:44.440] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002103)  
Pero sadly, sir, sa isang maliit na isla, kagaya ng Hibusong na hindi po pwede magdepend sa Facebook,

**[01:53:51.760] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002104)  
hindi po pwede ang notice nyo po.

**[01:53:54.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002105)  
sa amin to say that there is no expiration or as of September 18, 2014, has been suspended through a Facebook post.

**[01:54:07.920] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002106)  
Hindi ko rin po nakita si MGB Director, sir, to even visit Dinagat Islands.

**[01:54:14.140] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002107)  
In fact, I don't see anyone from there who visits the small island of Dinagat to update us on the status of mining application.

**[01:54:22.480] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002108)  
Pero magpapasalamat lang ako kay Usec.

**[01:54:24.980] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002109)  
Kasi sinalubong niya ako, Usec. Fragada.

**[01:54:27.960] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002110)  
At sinalubong niya po ako para sabihin na maybe we can expand even the discussion of Hibusong to go into the other mining applications who are marami din naman po.

**[01:54:38.800] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002111)  
So, sir, pakisagot naman, magkano po ang pwede mo nating asahan para po sa monitoring ng mining operations ng Dinagat na napaka, hindi naman po konti.

**[01:54:50.500] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002112)  
In fact, I can say more than ten.

**[01:54:53.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002113)  
Secretary?

**[01:54:54.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002114)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:54:55.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002115)  
On the aspect of the budget, I defer to Usec. Ana Teh of Finance.

**[01:55:00.820] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002116)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:55:02.900] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002117)  
Mr. Chair, for Caraga, we have a budget of 12.29 million for the whole region.

**[01:55:08.840] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002118)  
So, there's no specific breakdown how much is dedicated for Dinagat Island because there's no PENRA yet in Dinagat Island.

**[01:55:18.640] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002119)  
But, Mr. Chair, what we can do is that there's also a budget allocation.

**[01:55:23.380] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002120)  
For other activities within the region which we can actually utilize to ensure that there's proper monitoring on the different environmental activities in the island, Mr. Chair.

**[01:55:36.380] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-002121)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:55:37.440] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002122)  
May I respond?

**[01:55:37.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002123)  
May I allow you to make a rejoinder?

**[01:55:40.860] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002124)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:55:41.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002125)  
In fact, yun po ang magiging problema natin, Usec., dahil wala pong specific allocation for Dinagat.

**[01:55:50.140] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002126)  
Considering it's a very vulnerable island.

**[01:55:53.400] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002127)  
Napakarami pong areas that should be protected.

**[01:55:56.800] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002128)  
Pero po halimbawa, bonsai forest, may pending mining application sinuspending po ninyo.

**[01:56:03.520] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002129)  
Hindi rin po namin alam ano pong mangyayari.

**[01:56:05.540] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002130)  
Ano po ba?

**[01:56:06.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002131)  
Asa po ba kami isasara ito dahil bonsai forest po siya.

**[01:56:10.160] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002132)  
Tatlo lang po ang bonsai forest sa buong Pilipinas kasama ang Dinagat Islands.

**[01:56:14.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002133)  
Pero hanggang ngayon may MPSA application po, Sec.

**[01:56:18.280] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002134)  
At in fact, may declaration na ang DENR na sana po ay hindi matuloy ang mining dyan.

**[01:56:24.240] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002135)  
Dahilan po, ang threat sa amin, araw-araw, at nagkaka-declare lang po ng aming LGU ng State of Calamity

**[01:56:31.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002136)  
kasi 17,000 na po ang naapektuhan ng absence ng tubig.

**[01:56:37.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002137)  
Kasi kung dito po ay baha ang issue, doon po ay wala po kaming tubig dahil sa sobrang init.

**[01:56:42.120] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002138)  
At ang pinagbibintangan po kayo sa amin, na ang dahilan po ay dahil napuputol ang lahat ng puno sa aming mga bukid.

**[01:56:50.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002139)  
Pero kung lack of education and information,

**[01:56:54.280] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002140)  
wala po kaming makakausap kasi wala po kayong opisyal na pumupunta sa amin to monitor.

**[01:56:59.840] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002141)  
Specifically, sir, can I ask that we talk about this?

**[01:57:03.840] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002142)  
I will wait. I will not accuse you of anything yet.

**[01:57:07.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002143)  
Pero can I ask lang po, una po, can somebody go there?

**[01:57:41.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002149)

**[01:58:05.880] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002153)

**[01:58:08.940] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-002154)

**[01:58:17.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002155)

**[01:58:25.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002158)

**[01:58:47.200] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002166)

**[01:58:50.180] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002167)

**[01:58:52.700] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002168)

**[01:58:57.220] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002169)

**[01:59:05.120] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002170)

**[01:59:14.660] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002171)

**[01:59:23.000] DENR Undersecretary for Field Operations and Line Bureaus Joselin Marcus E. Fragada** (asr-002173)

**[02:12:19.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002669)  
Thank you, Chair. Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat. Thank you very much to the DENR family.

**[02:12:27.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002677)  
First of all, I would like to express my support to the concerns of Congresuman Kakabagaw being Dinagatnon.

**[02:12:42.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002690)  
However, I would like also to manifest that in fairness to DENR Region 13 we have a number of people affected by the mining operation in Lurito.

**[02:13:18.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002715)  
As a matter

**[02:13:19.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002716)  
of fact,

**[02:13:20.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002731)  
So, the newly designated OIC of MGB Caraga, Engineer Glenn Swante, visited personally the area

**[02:13:35.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002746)  
and then they have also conducted a hearing in the area with the people, including the newly designated OIC of Regional Executive Director Marites Ocampo and the in charge of the Satellite Office were there with me.

**[02:14:02.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002774)  
So, I would like Mr. Chair, for the record, to commend Yusec Mark because he is only one ticks away.

**[02:14:19.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002791)  
So, para lang malinaw, Mr. Chair, isang sagot, isang tanong. Can the... as a reiteration to the issue raised by Congresswoman Kaka Bag-ao, can the DENR give an assurance and a commitment—dalawa na po iyan, commitment at saka assurance, kasi may magkaibang ano.

**[02:14:49.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002851)  
Na wala nang mangyayaring mining operation sa Hibusong.

**[02:14:56.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002852)  
Check.

**[02:14:58.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002853)  
Yes.

**[02:15:00.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002855)  
Mr. Chair, we can give that assurance, Mr. Chair.

**[02:15:02.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002856)  
Okay.

**[02:15:03.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002857)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:15:04.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002858)  
Can you also give an assurance and commitment na wala nang mangyayaring mining doon sa Bonsai area at sa watershed area na nireklamo ko.

**[02:15:21.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002859)  
Watershed area doon sa isang barangay sa Lurito.

**[02:15:29.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002860)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:15:30.240] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002861)  
We can give that assurance, Mr. Chair.

**[02:15:31.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002862)  
These are considered protected areas or no-go zones, Mr. Chair.

**[02:15:36.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002863)  
Yung malinaw talaga, categorical. Walang mangyayaring mining doon sa area na yan.

**[02:15:43.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002865)  
Kasi Watershed yan at saka Bonsai Forest.

**[02:15:47.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002866)  
Watershed, Bonsai Forest at saka protected area.

**[02:15:52.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002867)  
Paano yung minasakit?

**[02:15:54.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002868)  
Pagka minahan ng bayan, kamay-kamay lang.

**[02:15:56.060] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002869)  
Wala rin.

**[02:15:57.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002870)  
Pagka minahan ng bayan, dinedesignate naman sa proper area, Mr. Chair.

**[02:16:03.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002871)  
This one is not.

**[02:16:04.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002872)  
So, may commitment na may SN Dinagat.

**[02:16:07.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002873)  
But instead, no, it should be permanent.

**[02:16:10.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002874)  
Mayroon naman pong competent person na nandyan sa Dinagat ngayon.

**[02:16:16.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002875)  
You can make it permanent, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:16:20.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002876)  
Kasi, ah...

**[02:16:21.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002877)  
This person, the people there are regularly coordinating with the people.

**[02:16:30.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002878)  
Pag nakita nila.

**[02:16:33.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002879)  
Mahirapan po kayo doon sa 13 million na ano ninyo.

**[02:16:38.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002880)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:16:39.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002881)  
Actually, Mr. Chair, for the information of the body,

**[02:16:43.380] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002882)  
there is a existing provincial environment office on Dinagat Island.

**[02:16:49.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002883)  
Yung Penro office po nandun.

**[02:16:51.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002884)  
So, siguro kailangan ng augmentation lang.

**[02:16:53.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002885)  
Nang funding in terms of monitoring for monitoring purposes, Mr. Chair.

**[02:16:59.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002886)  
Thank you with that. Pero, in relation to your budget, na-mention ni Secretary yung budget,

**[02:17:05.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002888)  
nagtataka ako, bakit sa 22.9% of your budget is concentrated to the office of the Secretary?

**[02:17:14.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002889)  
Bakit po ganon, Mr. Chair?

**[02:17:18.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002890)  
Mr. Chair, thank you very much for the question.

**[02:17:21.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002891)  
Kaya, nakalagay po. Na, office of the Secretary, pero kasama po lahat ng regional offices doon under the office of the Secretary, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:28.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002893)  
Just for the... And also, the bureaus except the two-line bureaus, MGB and EMB, that have their own separate budget, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:38.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002895)  
I will take it as it is, no?

**[02:17:42.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002896)  
Nerisk ko lang yan, for the record, na based on your budget, almost 73% na sa office of the Secretary.

**[02:17:51.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002897)  
Now, to my next point,

**[02:17:54.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002898)  
gusto kong i-advance in po, that our Chairman, no?

**[02:17:59.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002899)  
Those who are always presiding now, Chairman of the Committee on Energy,

**[02:18:04.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002900)  
binibilisan po namin yung mabilis na, ayong sariling kuryente act para mag-install na.

**[02:18:12.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002901)  
Tapos, natapos na po namin yung Battery Energy Storage Systems Act, nasa sinit na po.

**[02:18:20.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002902)  
Is the DENR ready?

**[02:18:22.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002903)  
Sa disposal?

**[02:18:24.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002904)  
You must be ready.

**[02:18:25.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002905)  
Wala pa kayong ano ngayon.

**[02:18:27.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002906)  
Are you ready? With the disposal of this equipment and materials?

**[02:18:33.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002908)  
Battery, for example.

**[02:18:37.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002909)  
Yung mga ma-install na solar rooftop with battery, after 10 years yan, the battery will be discarded kasi papalitan.

**[02:18:47.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002910)  
What is the environmental impact?

**[02:18:49.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002911)  
Are they not ready to recycle that, or do we have recycling plants?

**[02:18:55.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002914)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[02:18:56.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002915)  
Offhand, before I defer to Atty. Matias of the EMB, we have treatment storage and disposal facilities,

**[02:19:03.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002916)  
TSD facilities kung tawagin po, that cater to these electronic waste and other waste.

**[02:19:10.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002917)  
But to elaborate more, EMB Director Mike Matias, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:15.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002918)  
Especially lithium batteries.

**[02:19:18.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002919)  
Kasi wala pa yung batteries made of salt dito sa atin.

**[02:19:22.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002920)  
Lithium pa lang ang karamihan.

**[02:19:24.600] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002921)  
Go ahead.

**[02:19:25.540] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002922)  
Yes, sir, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:26.880] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002923)  
Actually, sir, as mentioned by the Secretary, meron po tayong mga TSD facilities na nga dito po sa atin and we are accrediting and registering po sila.

**[02:19:36.280] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002924)  
But, sir, yung valid point po, not only kasi in terms of scale po, malalaki po ito.

**[02:19:42.660] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002925)  
As you've mentioned, sir, yung mga solar panels po natin, they have been in existence.

**[02:19:48.220] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002926)  
So, dapat po na magkaroon po tayo ng pag-aaral as to how we can properly dispose of these.

**[02:19:54.660] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002927)  
Before yung end life po nitong mga...

**[02:19:56.900] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002928)  
Solar panels per se is not harmless.

**[02:19:59.820] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002929)  
Yung glass on top, yung filament sa ilalim, yung filament sa ilalim yun ang medyo may problema.

**[02:20:06.940] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-002930)  
Madali man i-remove yun.

**[02:20:08.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002931)  
But, Congressman Dagooc is asking you whether are you ready na mag-influx yung napakaraming lithium batteries.

**[02:20:17.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002932)  
Kasi yung 10 years, hindi man, masyadong matagal yun.

**[02:20:20.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002933)  
Ang iba, naka-install na yan eh.

**[02:20:22.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002934)  
Going 5 years, going 6 years na.

**[02:20:24.200] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002935)  
So, in the next 2 or 3 years, meron na yung mga lithium batteries to be disposed of.

**[02:20:29.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002936)  
So, as they're not ready, Visayas and Mindanao, saan ba ito mangyayari?

**[02:20:34.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002937)  
Kasi kung wala, kung wala namang kayong personnel in order to go around.

**[02:20:40.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002938)  
Remember, the rooftop solar and the batteries in relation to the question of Representative Nagooc is universal.

**[02:20:51.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002939)  
Philippine-wide yan, nationwide yan.

**[02:20:53.960] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002940)  
All I love...

**[02:20:56.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002941)  
So, how do you cope up with that?

**[02:20:58.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002942)  
It's an environmental nightmare kung saan-saan mo lang masipa ito sa kalsada, sa kanal, nakatiwangwang dun.

**[02:21:04.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002943)  
Di ba? Especially the lithium.

**[02:21:06.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002944)  
Okay? Thank you.

**[02:21:09.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002945)  
Mr. Cherno, naintindihan ko, mahirap po sagutin yan.

**[02:21:15.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002946)  
Kaya nga, sabi ko kanina, in advance ko na.

**[02:21:18.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002947)  
Kasi, just imagine in Metro Manila, kahit 30% lang ng kabahayan dito,

**[02:21:26.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002948)  
saan mo, din, nakikita mo yung inaugurate ni Presidente, napakalaking mga battery storage facilities.

**[02:21:34.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002949)  
Wala pa po tayong batas ngayon yan.

**[02:21:37.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002950)  
Yung base namin, nandun pa.

**[02:21:41.060] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002951)  
So, in advance ko yan, pag-isipan na ninyo, dito sa budget ninyo, Mr. Sekretary,

**[02:21:47.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002952)  
I will humbly request na i-consider ninyo, kasi mangyayari yan. And, kasi because of the high cost of electricity that we have now, kaya nagkaroon tayo ng mga batas na mapagaan yung ano, one is the sariling kurinti, kanya-kanya na tayong install.

**[02:22:06.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002956)  
So, at malaki po ang trabaho na gagawin ng DENR.

**[02:22:10.060] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002957)  
Now, no need to answer, kasi mahirap yun sagutin.

**[02:22:16.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002958)  
Now, on the next point, Mr. Chair, may problema tayo, Mr. Chair,

**[02:22:23.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002960)  
mga tao na nagtatrabaho in one of the mining companies in Dinagat.

**[02:22:29.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002961)  
Napuntahan na nila, but until now, hindi po nasasahuran.

**[02:22:32.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002962)  
May kaso na po ito.

**[02:22:33.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002963)  
Can the Secretary, in the Secretary level,

**[02:22:39.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002964)  
assure this representation na while nagkaso yung mining company at saka yung outsource niya,

**[02:22:47.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002965)

**[02:22:53.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002966)

**[02:22:55.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002967)  
Kasi sa ngayon, wala. Napaka-unfair naman yun. Pinagtatrabaho nila yun.

**[02:23:02.700] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002968)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:23:04.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002969)  
We will request not only the regional office of MGB,

**[02:23:09.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002970)  
but the central office to coordinate with this particular mining company, Mr. Chair,

**[02:23:13.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-002971)  
to assure that the workers are properly compensated.

**[02:23:20.760] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-002972)  
Ano ba 'yan, Cong. Dagooc? Pilipino ba 'yan?

**[02:23:23.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002973)  
Kasi karamihan lang sa Surigao Mining, puro na Chinese eh.

**[02:23:27.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002974)  
Kilala ni Secretary yung I don't want to mention.

**[02:23:31.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002975)  
Ang gusto ko lang mangyari, kasi nagtuturo na yung mining company na yung service provider niya ang dapat magbayad. Ngayon, kinasuhanan niya dahil hindi nagbayad.

**[02:23:46.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002977)  
My request to the Secretary is DENR's intervention.

**[02:23:51.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002978)  
Na makausap to... Nakausap tong mining company na while nagkaso sila,

**[02:23:56.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002980)  
ay kahit mag-parcel payment muna sila,

**[02:24:00.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002981)  
kasi walang, hindi nakapagtrabaho yung mga tao doon.

**[02:24:02.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002982)  
Dahil doon nagtatrabaho sa mining. Kumita na sila. Hinakot na nila yung or.

**[02:24:08.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002984)  
So, can the Secretary give an assurance for the intervention of DENR

**[02:24:13.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002985)

**[02:39:41.990] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003033)

**[02:39:53.240] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003036)

**[02:40:04.680] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003037)

**[02:40:07.880] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003038)

**[02:40:12.580] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003039)

**[02:40:15.620] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003040)

**[02:40:17.860] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003041)

**[02:40:21.340] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003042)

**[02:40:28.380] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003043)

**[02:40:33.660] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003044)

**[02:40:38.040] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003045)

**[02:40:42.040] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003046)

**[02:40:44.460] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003047)

**[02:40:47.200] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003048)

**[02:40:51.660] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003049)

**[02:40:53.440] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003050)

**[02:40:57.660] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003051)

**[02:41:02.760] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003052)

**[02:41:08.900] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003053)

**[02:41:10.700] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003054)

**[02:41:12.320] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-003055)

**[02:48:41.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003216)

**[02:48:44.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003217)  
Sige, pang-apat po.

**[02:48:47.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003218)  
Pang-apat naman po sa Zambales, yung tungkol sa exclusive dredging zones in Zambales rivers.

**[02:48:54.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003219)  
I wish to know about the status of China Harbor Engineering Company, or CHEC.

**[02:49:04.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003220)

**[02:49:12.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003221)  
in the West Philippine Sea.

**[02:49:13.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003222)  
Gusto ko pong malaman, is it still currently operating

**[02:49:16.360] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003223)  
under a valid D&R permit for dredging?

**[02:49:19.660] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003225)  
Kasi ang reports po namin, it has been confirmed as operating seven dredging vessels in the Santo Tomas River area.

**[02:49:28.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003229)  
Lagi hong may mga reports nakakarating sa opisina namin

**[02:49:31.600] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003230)  
about this, that almost daily there are big, huge vessels.

**[02:49:38.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003233)  
Di ba? Sa dredging. So why are these being allowed? And environmental

**[02:49:41.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003236)  
groups allege that the extracted sand

**[02:49:45.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003237)

**[02:49:51.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003239)

**[02:50:02.620] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003243)

**[02:50:11.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003246)

**[02:50:35.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003255)

**[02:50:37.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003256)  
Thank you very much for your questions, Mr. Chair.

**[02:50:39.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003257)  
On the first question, Mr. Chair, as we know,

**[02:50:45.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003258)  
there is an existing prohibition on the cutting of mangroves.

**[02:50:50.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003259)  
Yes. Yes.

**[02:50:52.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003261)  
So, the method that is allowed is to earthball the mangroves.

**[02:51:04.860] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003262)  
Right now, as mentioned earlier, Mr. Chair, the central office led by Yusek Mark Fragada and Yusek Ernesto Adobo, Jr. had a meeting with the staff of the good congresswoman, Mr. Chair,

**[02:51:22.660] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003265)  
and we explained the fact that this memo is an offshoot of many requests because at that time, there was a suspension on the issuance of earthball. There was a suspension on the issuance of earthball permits issued by the Biodiversity Management Bureau at that time.

**[02:51:44.660] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003268)  
But this took quite a while with no clear end in sight at that time.

**[02:51:55.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003269)  
And there were so many requests for government projects for earthballing permits for the project to continue.

**[02:52:06.340] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003271)  
Just to clarify, inform the body, Mr. Chair,

**[02:52:12.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003273)  
that in the DNR, we follow the hierarchy of mitigation for proposed projects.

**[02:52:20.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003274)  
First, number one is avoidance.

**[02:52:26.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003275)  
If we can avoid cutting anything, trees, mangroves, anything,

**[02:52:35.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003276)  
that is a choice that we will make.

**[02:52:36.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003277)  
But if this is not possible,

**[02:52:38.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003278)  
due to whatever reason, the design or the project proposal,

**[02:52:45.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003279)  
then we have minimization.

**[02:52:47.920] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003280)  
So the least number that we need to cut or to earthball, then that is what we will do.

**[02:52:55.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003282)  
Then the third is rehabilitation or restoration, Mr. Chair.

**[02:53:00.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003283)  
And the last is offsetting.

**[02:53:03.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003284)  
So this is an internationally recognized hierarchy of mitigation.

**[02:53:08.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003285)  
That is, faith, Инformation, provisions that are used worldwide.

**[02:53:12.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003289)  
So, going back, Mr. Chair,

**[02:53:15.360] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003290)  
we had many requests from the DOTR and DPWH,

**[02:53:22.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003291)  
and other government agencies and even private entities for earthballing.

**[02:53:27.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003292)  
So,

**[02:53:28.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003293)  
as Undersecretary for Field Operations,

**[02:53:32.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003294)  
We sat down with the Ecosystems Research and Development Bureau, which is our research arm, ERDB, of course, along with BNB, the Biodiversity Management Bureau, and we requested them to come up with a study on the earth boiling of mangroves, particularly on the survival rate, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:02.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003295)  
And the first phase was already completed, and the second phase is ongoing.

**[02:54:16.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003296)  
Because the study is differing with regard to the size of the mangroves that are undergoing the study, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:22.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003297)  
So, right now, the second phase of the study is approaching.

**[02:54:30.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003298)  
It is projected to be completed by December of this year, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:37.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003299)  
And with the result of that study, we will be coming up with a policy in the early part of 2027.

**[02:54:44.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003300)  
Now, we have created a technical working group to work on this policy moving forward, and we have included members of civil society, Mr. Chair, along with the DNR, the Ecosystems Research and Development Bureau.

**[02:55:05.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003301)  
The Development Bureau, ERDB, and BNB, and also members of civil society, particularly WWF, the World Wildlife Fund, and the Forest Foundation of the Philippines, and two more NGOs, Mr. Chair.

**[02:55:27.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003302)  
So, that is where we are right now, Mr. Chair.

**[02:55:30.780] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003303)  
Mr. Chair, shall I respond already?

**[02:55:33.100] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003304)  
Not yet.

**[02:55:33.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003305)  
Please, answer number two.

**[02:55:36.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003306)

**[02:56:19.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003321)

**[02:56:41.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003327)

**[02:56:43.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003329)

**[02:56:51.740] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003331)

**[02:56:54.900] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003333)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:56:56.220] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003334)

**[02:57:16.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003338)

**[02:57:24.900] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003340)  
In Mindoro, in South Cotabado, which is Tampacan.

**[02:57:29.240] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003341)  
Mindoro?

**[02:57:31.280] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003342)  
Mindoro. Mindoro?

**[02:57:32.900] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003344)  
Mindoro. There is no mining there.

**[02:57:34.880] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003346)  
Yes, that is why they are not operating.

**[02:57:37.220] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003347)

**[02:57:39.540] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003349)

**[02:57:40.760] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003351)  
Another one is in the area of Pangasinan La Union, which is a mix of offshore and onshore

**[02:57:45.260] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003352)  
areas.

**[02:57:46.860] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003353)  
Also not operating.

**[02:57:50.140] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003354)  
Another one is in Palawan, which was cancelled, but under a court litigation in Palawan.

**[02:57:52.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003355)  
Those are the four non-operating.

**[02:57:59.220] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003356)  
All in all, we have six FTAAs.

**[02:58:04.700] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003357)  
In the question of renewal of Tampacan, all of our FTAA contracts, there is a provision

**[02:58:14.400] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003358)  
that if a suspension is attributable to force majeure, they can enjoy the period of non-operation

**[02:58:23.660] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003359)  
because of force majeure and we can extend it.

**[02:58:26.880] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003360)  
There are instances like that.

**[02:58:29.800] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003366)  
Contractors are availing of that provision that has been approved by the DNR and even the President of the Philippines when it comes to the FTAAs. That is the status of the FTAA.

**[02:58:44.600] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003369)
