**[03:01:16.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003424)  
Your Honors, partially, Mr. Chair.

**[03:01:17.980] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003425)  
With respect to the...

**[03:01:19.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003426)  
Earthballing.

**[03:01:21.980] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003427)  
Yes, we understood now that there's a TWG, but I think this is recently created.

**[03:01:28.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003428)  
Just recently created.

**[03:01:29.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003429)  
So, I'm wondering why that memorandum was issued at all, even before consultations or even before TWG or scientific study.

**[03:01:39.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003430)  
Kasi yung survival rate po ata, that's something that is still put into question.

**[03:01:45.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003431)  
What exactly is the survival rate?

**[03:01:47.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003432)  
Di ho ba?

**[03:01:48.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003433)  
So, all of these things should have been...

**[03:01:50.140] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003434)  
confirmed first or verified first before allowing the issuance of this memorandum.

**[03:01:55.660] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003435)  
Yes, it is considered interim operational guidance, but is it really interim or is it a de facto permanent policy na po ngayon?

**[03:02:05.280] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003436)  
Yung pag-a-allow ng, you know, earthballing for the flagship infrastructure?

**[03:02:13.600] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003437)  
Very briefly.

**[03:02:14.560] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003438)  
Mr. Chair, if I may?

**[03:02:17.720] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003440)  
Actually, as I said earlier, there were a lot of requests for government projects.

**[03:02:25.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003442)  
And

**[03:02:26.240] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003439)  
we deemed it appropriate at the time under the sustainable development that we are following.

**[03:02:50.740] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003443)  
To come up with this interim memorandum or interim guidelines through a memorandum,

**[03:02:54.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003444)  
specifying certain methods or guidelines that had to be followed after consultation with the ERDB,

**[03:03:00.200] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003445)  
the Ecosystems Research and Development Bureau, the BMB, and even the Forest Management Bureau.

**[03:03:08.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003446)  
Kasi based on the reports of our technical personnel,

**[03:03:15.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003447)  
bumaba ba ang survival rate ng...

**[03:03:16.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003448)  
earthballing for several reasons, for several factors.

**[03:03:25.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003449)  
One is, minamadali yung project. So, basta tinatanggal na lang.

**[03:03:32.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003451)  
It takes time and there's a particular method that should be used in extracting these plants to be transferred,

**[03:03:44.240] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003452)  
these trees to be transferred. That's one.

**[03:03:46.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003454)  
Secondly, yung site... identification is another factor.

**[03:03:51.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003456)  
So, kailangan maghanap talaga ng area where these earthballed trees are transferred.

**[03:04:02.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003457)  
For instance, meron po kaming case in point in the city of Manila.

**[03:04:08.040] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003458)  
Merong na earthball po na ilang puno doon inilipat sa malapit sa post office.

**[03:04:17.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003459)  
And we have... We have already seen na tumubo na po sa... It grew already from the time...

**[03:04:23.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003462)  
That is not mangrove.

**[03:04:24.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003464)  
Pero ibig sabihin, in terms of... It's another... Earthballing lang po. Yeah, earthballing in general.

**[03:04:33.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003468)  
So, actually, we're encouraged by the initial results that have been sent to us already.

**[03:04:40.220] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003469)  
But for us to be able to really extensively complete the study, we are targeting December of this year, Mr. Chair.

**[03:04:51.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003472)  
Thank you for that. But I'm not very sure about the encouraging results of the study. I think we have to be more... DNR has to be more forthright about that.

**[03:05:01.400] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003476)  
The results of the scientific study on the earthballing and survival rate,

**[03:05:05.820] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003477)  
masyado atang mababa.

**[03:05:07.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003478)  
Kung hindi man zero percent, I know...

**[03:05:11.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003479)  
Kasi, umang Dilima, would you want to get some advice from an expert from the chair?

**[03:05:15.740] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003480)  
Sure, sir.

**[03:05:16.760] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003481)  
Okay, Mr. DNR, Secretary.

**[03:05:19.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003482)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:05:21.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003483)  
As far as mangrove is concerned, no need to ball earth.

**[03:05:26.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003484)  
Please listen to me.

**[03:05:28.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003485)  
In Malaysia, mangroves are allowed to be cut and utilized.

**[03:05:33.420] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003486)  
Instead of earthballing the mangrove, which is hastily done,

**[03:05:36.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003487)  
and I have not seen a good survival rate,

**[03:05:40.100] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003488)  
if it is land-based, acacia or manga,

**[03:05:43.960] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003489)  
yan, madali po nating earthball.

**[03:05:46.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003490)  
Bubuhay yan.

**[03:05:47.520] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003491)  
Pero mangrove, mahirap po buhayin.

**[03:05:50.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003493)  
Instead, ipinalize mo na lang yung Bureau of Concern

**[03:05:53.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003494)  
or yung private individual, private contractor,

**[03:05:56.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003495)  
pabayarin mo in order to, kalimbawa,

**[03:06:00.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003496)  
1,000 square meters yung ma-affected na earthball mo na mangrove,

**[03:06:06.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003497)  
you penalize them for a 1-hectare plantation ng mangrove,

**[03:06:11.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003498)  
pabayarin mo sila,

**[03:06:12.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003499)  
and that is more effective rather than earthballing 1,000.

**[03:06:16.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003500)  
That's my advice.

**[03:06:17.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003501)  
I know very well because I'm a forester.

**[03:06:19.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003502)  
Thank you, Madam Representative de Lima,

**[03:06:23.580] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003503)  
and thank you, Secretary.

**[03:06:24.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003504)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, for that suggestion.

**[03:06:28.100] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003505)  
But in this particular instance, Mr. Chair, we are constrained by the fact that it is a law that prohibits the cutting.

**[03:06:40.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003507)  
So that's where we are. We need to address it through legislation if we are to allow the cutting of mangroves moving forward. But anyway,

**[03:06:48.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003511)  
over and above that, as I mentioned earlier in the hierarchy of mitigation, talagang required naman sila mag-offset. Even if we allow the earthballing,

**[03:06:58.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003515)  
they are required to provide offsetting.

**[03:07:08.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003516)  
So magpa-plant po talaga sila ng mangroves.

**[03:07:13.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003517)  
So that's in the guidelines.

**[03:07:15.100] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003518)  
Is that in the guidelines?

**[03:07:16.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003519)  
But again, I think,

**[03:07:19.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003520)  
momentarily, Your Honors,

**[03:07:20.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003521)  
I wish to suggest that to suspend first the implementation,

**[03:07:23.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003522)  
if not altogether, do away.

**[03:07:25.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003523)  
There's a request to suspend first earthballing on mangroves.

**[03:07:27.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003524)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[03:07:30.500] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003525)  
what say you?

**[03:07:32.740] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003526)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:07:35.420] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003527)  
with all due respect to the good Congresswoman,

**[03:07:38.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003528)  
we will evaluate that suggestion.

**[03:07:40.520] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003529)  
But as you know,

**[03:07:41.880] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003530)  
there are a lot of developments that are ongoing,

**[03:07:45.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003531)  
so we need to be able to also balance.

**[03:07:48.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003532)  
Moving forward.

**[03:07:50.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003533)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:50.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003534)  
Thank you.

**[03:07:51.520] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003535)  
On the second issue, just a short comment po.

**[03:07:54.200] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003536)  
Yes, please go ahead.

**[03:07:55.480] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003537)  
Doon sa Chinese nationals,

**[03:07:56.940] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003538)  
nakikita po natin,

**[03:07:57.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003539)  
wala pang isang financier,

**[03:07:59.300] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003540)  
wala pang isang sindikato

**[03:08:00.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003541)

**[03:08:04.060] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003542)  
Bagkus, deportation lang

**[03:08:05.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003543)  
ang mga nagiging

**[03:08:07.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003544)

**[03:08:08.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003545)  
Sanction.

**[03:08:10.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003546)  
But deportation is not accountability.

**[03:08:13.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003547)  
Paulit-ulit lang po. Ilang beses pa po na dapat mangyari ito bago tayo tulungan. Ito po yung mag-imbestiga ng masusi

**[03:08:20.800] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003551)  
kung sino talaga ang mga nasa likod ng pag-employ o paggagamit ng mga Chinese nationals sa ating mga mining

**[03:08:28.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003555)  
when it's absolutely prohibited under the Constitution.

**[03:08:31.360] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003557)  
Doon naman po sa third issue, doon sa FTAA.

**[03:08:34.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003559)  
So I understand that there are two active FTAAs, pero mukhang hindi ko po narinig yung sagot

**[03:08:39.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003561)  
tungkol sa renewal nila.

**[03:08:44.140] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003562)  
I suppose there's two active FTAAs

**[03:08:45.360] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003563)  
that have undergone renewal already.

**[03:08:51.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003564)  
And then the follow-up question to that

**[03:08:53.440] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003565)  
is that sa renewal ho ba nagkaroon din ng consultation?

**[03:08:56.140] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003567)  
Kasi parang nagiging posisyon

**[03:08:59.400] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003568)  
ng DENR is that pagdating sa renewal

**[03:09:02.500] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003570)  
hindi na kailangan ng consultation.

**[03:09:04.140] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003571)  
A position that I totally disagree with.

**[03:09:06.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003572)  
Sorry, please reply. And that would be the last retort.

**[03:09:14.320] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003574)  
Doon po sa Oceana Gold, there was a renewal conduct during that time.

**[03:09:23.300] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003577)  
May public consultation naman po.

**[03:09:25.640] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003578)  
May public consultation.

**[03:09:27.260] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003579)  
Pero on the stage of approving

**[03:09:31.500] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003580)  
the declaration of mining project feasibility.

**[03:09:33.780] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003581)  
Doon sa specific guidelines po natin, sa batas po, IRR, yung pong approval ng mining project by the LGU and the community affected is number one, doon sa public scoping

**[03:09:55.380] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003587)  
when it entered into EIA system sa ECC. And number two is when we are approved the mining project under the declaration of mining project feasibility.

**[03:10:03.780] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003593)  
Yun po yung compliance nila po.

**[03:10:06.260] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003594)  
Doon sa

**[03:10:07.860] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003595)  
renewal po, for as long as

**[03:10:09.540] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003596)  
wala naman pong

**[03:10:12.500] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003597)  
wala naman pong continuing operation

**[03:10:13.460] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003598)  
naman po ang pinag-uusapan,

**[03:10:15.740] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003599)  
hindi na po natin nirequire yung

**[03:10:17.740] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003600)  
LGU endorsement kasi pre-approved.

**[03:10:19.820] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003601)  
Nag-improve na po yung mining operation nila

**[03:10:21.020] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-003602)  
under the MPF po.

**[03:10:23.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003603)  
Okay na.

**[03:10:25.140] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003604)  
Thank you.

**[03:10:26.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003605)  
Thank you,

**[03:10:27.920] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003606)  
Congressman Delima.

**[03:10:29.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003607)  
I'd like to

**[03:10:32.400] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003608)

**[03:10:33.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003609)  
Congressman Brian po

**[03:10:35.160] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003610)  
to ask one question.

on transparency and

**[03:11:01.340] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003624)

**[03:11:02.840] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003625)  
and your updates regarding

**[03:11:04.960] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003626)

**[03:11:11.200] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003629)

**[03:11:13.060] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003630)  
Now, Mr. Chair, for the question

**[03:11:15.380] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003631)  
that I want to ask of the agency,

**[03:11:16.880] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003632)  
the President issued a

**[03:11:19.100] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003633)

**[03:11:21.520] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003634)

**[03:11:24.300] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003635)

**[03:11:40.100] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003639)

**[03:11:41.600] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003640)

**[03:11:49.360] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003642)

**[03:11:52.920] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003644)  
May I ask, because a lot of our

**[03:11:54.940] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003645)

**[03:11:57.240] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003646)

**[03:11:58.660] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003647)  
important mining operations,

**[03:12:01.040] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003648)  
including Pax Silica,

**[03:12:02.680] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003649)

**[03:12:05.000] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003650)

**[03:12:08.720] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003652)  
to protect our

**[03:12:11.140] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003653)

**[03:12:13.220] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003654)

**[03:12:15.280] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-003655)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:12:16.540] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003657)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[03:12:20.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003658)  
Before I turn over, if I may,

**[03:12:22.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003659)  
Mr. Chair, to Asec

**[03:12:24.840] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003660)  
Mike Cabalda of

**[03:12:26.440] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003661)  
Mining Concerns, we have

**[03:12:28.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003662)  
existing policies,

**[03:12:30.640] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003663)  
Mr. Chair, right now, that are

**[03:12:32.600] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003664)

For us to be able

**[03:13:56.800] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003706)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:13:58.460] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003707)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating

**[03:14:01.100] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003708)  
lahat.

**[03:14:01.860] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003709)  
Cong. Brian po,

**[03:14:04.220] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003710)  
let me just explain by saying

**[03:14:06.120] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003711)  
yung ating

**[03:14:07.880] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003712)  
paggawa ng ating EO192,

**[03:14:10.840] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003713)  
very high

**[03:14:12.560] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003714)  
ang ating pananaw

**[03:14:14.420] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003715)  
pagdating doon sa

**[03:14:15.340] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003716)  
pagproteksyon ng ating environment.

**[03:14:18.540] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003717)  
Kaya pag tignan po po yung

**[03:14:20.340] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003718)  
executive order, mataas

**[03:14:22.800] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003719)  
ang ating standard na hinahanap

**[03:14:24.540] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003720)  
para sa mga kumpanyang papasok

**[03:14:26.700] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003721)  
dito sa critical minerals.

**[03:14:28.160] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003722)  
And I say critical minerals.

**[03:14:31.560] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003723)  
ESG

**[03:14:32.200] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003724)  
is very much highlighted doon sa EO.

**[03:14:34.920] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003725)  
So, hindi ibig sabihin

**[03:14:36.960] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003726)  
po doon sa seksyon na

**[03:14:38.240] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003727)  
pinabibilis po natin ang ating permitting

**[03:14:40.760] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003728)  
na magiging baliwala

**[03:14:42.580] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003729)  
na po ang ating environmental

**[03:14:44.320] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003730)  
and social requirements.

**[03:14:46.800] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003731)  
Mas paiigtingin pa po natin

**[03:14:48.760] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003732)  
actually.

**[03:14:50.160] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003733)  
Dahil nga po, ito naman ay hindi po parang shotgun policy that would allow for all minerals to be mined.

**[03:14:58.300] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003734)  
We now define minerals as critical, meaning these are the minerals that would be used for energy transition

**[03:15:04.280] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003735)  
and all the things that we're really ano listening right now.

**[03:15:08.560] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003736)  
And in fact, doon sa ating lalabas na, because this particular law has been published na sa official gazette,

**[03:15:16.280] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003737)  
yung aming timeline already started.

**[03:15:19.620] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003738)  
For the first 30 days, in fact, we're supposed to be coming up with a critical minerals list.

**[03:15:25.500] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003739)  
And that critical minerals list would now define which projects would now be covered by this particular executive order.

**[03:15:32.980] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003740)  
And we have something like, in fact, Mr. Chairman, prior to the President signing this executive order,

**[03:15:41.520] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003741)  
we have done our work already ahead of it, anticipating that it would be signed.

**[03:15:45.940] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003742)  
Primarily because there is really a need for us to have a unified, structured, directional strategy on how we can mine our critical minerals.

**[03:15:57.680] DENR Assistant Secretary for Mining Concerns Michael V. Cabalda** (asr-003743)  
Thank you.

**[03:15:59.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003744)  
Sige.

**[03:16:01.200] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003745)

**[03:16:04.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003746)  
So please submit your policy direction towards that.

**[03:16:08.400] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003747)

**[03:16:17.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003749)

**[03:16:23.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003750)  
Didiretso na rin ako, dahil ang pansin naman namin sa budget proposal ninyo ay to conduct EIAs ay meron po kayong P251 million.

**[03:16:35.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003751)  
Correct po ba?

**[03:16:37.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003752)  
Environmental Impact Assessment. Para po magawa siya.

**[03:16:41.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003753)  
P251 million.

**[03:16:43.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003754)

**[03:16:48.785] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003755)

**[03:16:51.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003757)

**[03:16:53.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003758)  
Pero kaya naman nga po tinatanong natin dahil critical yung work ng DNR sa pag-ensure na kung ano pong mga plano ay hindi po ito makakasagabal, makakapinsala sa kalikasan.

**[03:17:07.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003759)

**[03:17:14.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003760)

**[03:17:17.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003761)

**[03:17:21.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003762)  
Sinisimula na nga po sa Capas, Tarlac yung pagkuha ng mga lupa ng mga lokal na komunidad na nakatira doon.

**[03:17:28.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003763)

**[03:17:42.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003764)

**[03:17:46.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003765)  
Wala.

**[03:17:47.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003766)  
Wala pa.

**[03:17:48.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003767)

**[03:17:51.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003768)

**[03:17:52.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003769)

**[03:17:54.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003770)

**[03:18:03.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003771)

**[03:18:10.200] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003772)

**[03:18:15.980] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003773)

**[03:18:22.080] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003774)

**[03:18:29.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003775)  
Wala pa.

**[03:18:30.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003776)

**[03:18:31.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003777)  
Dahil po, nung July 23, ang BCDA, sabi niya po na may master plan sila.

**[03:18:37.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003778)  
At inaprubahan na raw po ng DNR yung master plan na ito.

**[03:18:41.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003779)

**[03:18:43.640] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003780)

**[03:18:45.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003781)  
EMB?

**[03:18:45.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003782)  
Mr. Chair, if I may...

**[03:18:47.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003783)

**[03:18:53.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003784)

**[03:18:57.840] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003785)  
Mr. Chair, I think the good Congresswoman is referring to the approved ECC that was issued for the development plan of the BCDA,

**[03:19:09.140] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003786)  
which covers the entire area.

**[03:19:11.240] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003787)  
But if we're going to look at yung projects, sir, as mentioned in the reports, yung 1,600 hectares,

**[03:19:19.340] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003788)  
wala pa pong specific projects that will be located po dun sa area.

**[03:19:25.560] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003789)  
So, may ECC na, pero wala pang Environmental Impact Assistant?

**[03:19:32.780] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003790)  
Ang mangyayari po dyan, sir, if there will be applications for projects within that 1,600,

**[03:19:39.320] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003791)  
then they will be required to undergo an EIA.

**[03:19:42.060] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003792)  
So, they will be required to secure an ECC for that specific project.

**[03:19:47.340] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003793)  
For example po, may nauna.

**[03:19:48.660] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003794)  
Um, um, um, factory.

**[03:19:51.920] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003795)  
Ang assessment po na gagawin natin, when they file an application for ECC for that factory,

**[03:19:57.520] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003796)  
we will, it will undergo consultation and environmental impact assessment of that particular.

**[03:20:03.080] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003797)  
And if subsequently po, kasi ang pagkakaintindi po namin,

**[03:20:08.340] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003798)  
yung mga locators po dito, hindi siya pare-pareho, sabay-sabay na darating eh.

**[03:20:12.820] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003799)  
So, pag may susunod po, i-assess po namin yung environmental impact of that subsequent project,

**[03:20:18.920] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003800)  
in relation to that existing project that has already been covered by an EIA.

**[03:20:23.160] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003801)  
So, ito po yung cumulative impact assessment na gagawin natin,

**[03:20:27.440] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003802)  
so that all the impacts of those locators within that 1,600 hectares will be considered.

**[03:20:33.500] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003803)  
So, dahil, ayun, parang tingi-tingi na papasok ganyan,

**[03:20:39.340] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003804)  
wala pong pangkabuuang EIA na ginagawa ngayon yung DENR.

**[03:20:44.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003805)  
Hindi po ba dapat meron?

**[03:20:47.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003806)  
Supposedly, may master plan.

**[03:20:48.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003807)  
May mga locations, meron pong, hindi nga lang daw po ito New Clark City,

**[03:20:52.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003808)  
according to the PTI-BOI, pero other parts of the country po.

**[03:20:57.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003809)  
Alam po ba ito ng DNR?

**[03:20:59.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003810)  
And meron po ba siyang pinaplano?

**[03:21:01.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003811)  
Or may environmental management plan po ba na in the works sa DNR?

**[03:21:06.280] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003812)  
Thank you, Congresswoman.

**[03:21:08.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003813)  
Ang pag-assess po natin ng environmental impact,

**[03:21:11.900] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003814)  
we'll have to know first kung ano po yung mga specific projects na ilalagay.

**[03:21:16.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003815)  
So that we will understand, for example,

**[03:21:19.140] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003816)  
the water requirement, kung meron po ito,

**[03:21:21.400] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003817)  
electricity requirement.

**[03:21:22.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003818)  
Then, on the basis of that, as mentioned by the legal secretary,

**[03:21:26.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003819)  
yung ating project description,

**[03:21:28.480] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003820)  
i-assess po natin what are the impacts po

**[03:21:32.020] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003821)  
nung mga activities na yun.

**[03:21:34.580] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003822)  
Right now po, we do not have that specifics

**[03:21:37.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003823)  
so that we cannot properly have this environmental management plan

**[03:21:42.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003824)  
to address those anticipated impacts.

**[03:21:45.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003825)  
So once we get hold of those information po,

**[03:21:48.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003826)  
that's why we're coordinating then with the BCDA,

**[03:21:52.280] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003827)  
para at least yung mga anticipated impacts ng mga proposed projects.

**[03:21:57.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003828)  
But to set the record straight po,

**[03:22:00.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003829)  
as of now, wala pa po tayong specific projects na ilalagay po.

**[03:22:04.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003830)  
So to clarify po, Mr. Chair,

**[03:22:06.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003831)  
ano po yung na-issuehan nyo ng ECC?

**[03:22:10.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003832)

**[03:22:13.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003833)  
wala pang EIA na alam or ginagawa,

**[03:22:17.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003834)  
ano po?

**[03:22:18.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003835)  
Ano ba yan, Mr. Chair?

**[03:22:20.700] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003836)  
Ang issue ones po natin would be for the development of that area

**[03:22:26.180] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003837)  
within the Clark City.

**[03:22:28.800] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003838)

**[03:22:31.760] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003839)  
yun lang po ang iko-cover nun.

**[03:22:33.940] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003840)  
But as to the specific impacts of the projects that will be put there,

**[03:22:38.300] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003841)  
wala pa po tayo.

**[03:22:39.260] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003842)  
And rest assured that should there be any projects po,

**[03:22:43.000] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003843)  
dadaan po ito sa EIA process,

**[03:22:46.060] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-003844)  
which includes consultation and public hearings.

**[03:22:49.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003845)  
Pero ang narinig ko rin po kanina, Mr. Chair,

**[03:22:51.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003846)  
na bagong mag-issue ng ECC, dapat may consultations.

**[03:22:55.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003847)  
Pero ang report on the ground ay hindi naman po na-consult

**[03:22:58.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003848)  
yung mga communities na ma-apektohan sa New Clark City,

**[03:23:02.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003849)  
sa Capas, tungkol po sa gagawing Pax Silica initiative.

**[03:23:06.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003850)  
So bakit po may ECC na?

**[03:23:10.720] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003851)  
Ang pagkakaintindi, although the regional director can provide details po,

**[03:23:16.820] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003852)  
but ang understanding,

**[03:23:19.060] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003853)  
yung area ng Pax Silica is just part of the total area of the...

**[03:23:24.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003854)  
Total 1,600 hectares?

**[03:23:25.820] EMB Director Michael Drake P. Matias** (asr-003855)  
Yung 1,600 hectares is part of the...

**[03:23:28.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003856)  
Kasi in fact, yung Pax Silica, wala pang application yun,

**[03:23:31.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003857)  
wala pang katiyakan kung ano talagang mga factories ang lalagay nila doon.

**[03:23:36.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003858)  
Gano'n ba yun?

**[03:23:37.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003859)  
Nasaan yung RD?

**[03:23:38.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003860)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:23:39.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003861)  
Nandyan?

**[03:23:40.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003862)  
Lapitan mo, sagutin mo si Congresswoman Co.

**[03:23:42.940] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003863)  
Cong, magandang hapon po, Mr. Chair, for the question. To set the record straight, tama po na mayroong ECC yung master plan po ng New Clark City.

**[03:23:53.920] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003867)  
This is about 9,400 hectares po.

**[03:23:56.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003868)  
Industrial?

**[03:23:57.500] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003869)  
Yes, sir. May mga zoning po na inindicate doon.

**[03:24:00.440] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003870)  
Either commercial, mayroon pong pang-residential, mayroon pong pang-open parks, and so on.

**[03:24:07.180] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003871)  
9,000?

**[03:24:08.000] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003872)  
400, sir.

**[03:24:09.640] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003873)  
Ito po yung kabuuan ng New Clark City.

**[03:24:13.600] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003874)  
Ngayon po, doon po sa area ng 1,600 hectares,

**[03:24:17.500] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003875)  
base po doon sa master plan nila.

**[03:24:20.200] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003876)  
Wala pa pong nakalagay po doon.

**[03:24:22.340] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003877)  
So ito po, magiging subject po ito ng another EIA.

**[03:24:25.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003878)  
Another one.

**[03:24:27.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003879)  
So, thank you po sa pag-clarify.

**[03:24:30.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003880)  
Nakonsulta na po ba kayo ng BCDA or kung sino man,

**[03:24:34.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003881)  
kung saan po masasource yung tubig for the Pax Silica Manufacturing Hub

**[03:24:38.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003882)  
or kung ano man po yung gustong iplano doon?

**[03:24:40.440] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003883)  
As previously mentioned po, wala pa pong konkretong detalye na nasusumiti po sa atin.

**[03:24:45.160] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003884)  
In fact, yung EIA process po natin,

**[03:24:48.240] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003885)  
hindi pa po nagsisimula dahil wala pa pong konkretong mga detalye doon.

**[03:24:53.280] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003886)  
Pero sa ngayon po, mayroon pong mga coordination just to brief us

**[03:24:58.680] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003887)  
on what potentially could be the project.

**[03:25:01.020] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003888)  
Kasi wala pa rin pong malinaw na locators kung sino ang pupuesto doon.

**[03:25:05.840] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003889)  
Kasi ngayon po, puro intent pa lang po din ang nakukuha ni BCDA

**[03:25:09.440] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003890)  
from other potential locators according po sa information nila.

**[03:25:13.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003891)  
So, since sabi po ng DENR,

**[03:25:17.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003892)  
wala pa, ang tanong ko na lang po and request,

**[03:25:19.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003893)  
pwede po bang may written opinion ang DNR na ma-accomplish

**[03:25:25.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003894)  
on the environmental effects of a data center or a semiconductor manufacturing hub?

**[03:25:31.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003895)  
Kaya po ba yun na ma-accomplish?

**[03:25:34.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003896)  
So, hopefully before plenary po, para po ito na yung panimula kung ano man gagawin EIA.

**[03:25:39.460] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003897)  
Wala pong application eh.

**[03:25:41.260] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003898)  
Mr. Chair, yun nga po yung lalamanin ng ating EIA.

**[03:25:45.380] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003899)  
So, we would not like to preempt po sana

**[03:25:47.740] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003900)  
yung magiging pag-conduct po ng EIA natin

**[03:25:51.240] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003901)  
because everything would be dependent on the study to be conducted po.

**[03:25:54.780] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003902)  
But, rest assured ho, yung mga requirement ho ninyo on public participation, consultation,

**[03:26:00.720] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003903)  
part ho ito ng proseso na dapat ho talagang gawin.

**[03:26:03.400] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003904)  
At sisiguraduin ho natin na hindi ho,

**[03:26:05.720] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003905)  
kumbaga meron din ho social acceptability yan at kasama din ho doon

**[03:26:09.460] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003906)  
kung ano ho yung pinaplano ng proponent na dapat niyang gawin

**[03:26:12.940] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003907)  
para ho doon sa mga kababayan natin na maapektuhan doon sa lugar.

**[03:26:16.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003908)  
But this won't be,

**[03:26:17.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003909)  
pre-empting it,

**[03:26:18.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003910)  
I think, Mr. Chair,

**[03:26:19.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003911)  
sige, yung EIA pwede pong i-accomplish

**[03:26:21.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003912)  
kung ano man po yung study na kailangan i-accomplish.

**[03:26:25.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003913)  
Pero this one,

**[03:26:27.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003914)  
may data centers na po ba dito?

**[03:26:29.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003915)  
May manufacturing hubs na po ba dito?

**[03:26:30.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003916)  
This would be a written opinion lang.

**[03:26:32.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003917)  
Ano yung pwedeng effects environmentally neto?

**[03:26:36.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003918)  
Pwede po ba yun, Mr. Chair?

**[03:26:37.900] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003919)  
Mr. Chair, I think we can provide whatever information we have

**[03:26:41.700] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003920)  
kung anong existing natin.

**[03:26:43.140] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003921)  
Basta may application na yung facility.

**[03:26:44.820] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003922)  
Basta meron tayong konkretong information po.

**[03:26:46.920] DENR-EMB Region 3 Director Martin Jose V. Despi** (asr-003923)  
We will try our best, ma'am, to provide the needed information if we have it.

**[03:26:50.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003924)  
Okay. And kaya po ba before plenary, Mr. Chair?

**[03:26:54.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003925)  
In addition to that,

**[03:26:56.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003926)  
in addition to that for the information of the public,

**[03:26:59.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003927)  
yun namang Pax Silica,

**[03:27:02.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003928)  
this is a chance in a lifetime.

**[03:27:04.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003929)  
At yung tinatakot natin na 60 million liters

**[03:27:08.660] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003930)  
ang kailangan sa isang araw,

**[03:27:10.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003931)  
kung i-combine mo yung discharge ng all Zambales rivers

**[03:27:15.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003932)  
into the ocean,

**[03:27:17.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003933)  
every second is equivalent to that 60 million liters.

**[03:27:21.500] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003934)  
That's only equivalent to 60,000 cubic meters,

**[03:27:25.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003935)  
which is one panamax.

**[03:27:26.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003936)  
Parang itong building na ito.

**[03:27:28.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003937)  
Yan lang ang per second na lumalabas sa lahat ng ilog na Zambales.

**[03:27:31.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003938)  
Kaya huwag matakot yung ating publiko dito sa Pax Silica.

**[03:27:35.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003939)  
Just a small information.

**[03:27:39.880] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003940)  
Kasi water is my forte,

**[03:27:43.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003941)  
and 60 million liters is only,

**[03:27:47.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003942)  
60,000 cubic meters,

**[03:27:49.720] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003943)  
that is only one panamax.

**[03:27:51.500] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003944)  
E maliit po yung 60 million liters

**[03:27:53.620] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003945)  
compared to all the rivers in Zambales

**[03:27:57.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003946)  
discharging to the ocean

**[03:27:58.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003947)  
that's only one second.

**[03:28:00.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003948)  
Ganun po.

**[03:28:01.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003949)  
Kaya huwag matakot yung ating public

**[03:28:03.520] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003950)  
sa Pax Silica.

**[03:28:04.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003951)  
This is a chance in a lifetime.

**[03:28:07.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003952)  
We must get this.

**[03:28:09.240] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003953)  
Thank you.

**[03:28:09.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-003954)  
Please go ahead.

**[03:28:11.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003955)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:28:12.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003956)  
Pero with all due respect po,

**[03:28:14.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003957)  
marami pong may concern sa Pax Silica

**[03:28:16.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003958)  
dahil,

**[03:28:17.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003959)  
aside from tubig,

**[03:28:18.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003960)  
sa kuryente,

**[03:28:19.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003961)  
sa lupa,

**[03:28:20.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003962)  
marami pong compounded concerns yan.

**[03:28:22.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003963)  
And we're looking at DNR

**[03:28:23.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003964)  
to resolve some of these concerns.

**[03:28:26.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003965)  
And so,

**[03:28:27.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003966)  
yung concerns naman po ito,

**[03:28:28.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003967)  
hindi ito in a vacuum lang.

**[03:28:30.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003968)  
Everywhere else in the world,

**[03:28:32.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003969)  
may mga concerns na po on data centers.

**[03:28:34.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003970)  
I'll just cite a few studies, no?

**[03:28:36.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003971)  
So, for example,

**[03:28:38.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003972)  
sa US,

**[03:28:39.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003973)  
sa Amazon's Canton Facility, Mississippi,

**[03:28:42.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003974)  
ang sabi po,

**[03:28:43.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003975)  
we were promised prosperity

**[03:28:44.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003976)  
but got poisoned air and vanishing water.

**[03:28:47.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003977)  
May class action na po

**[03:28:48.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003978)  
since February 2026

**[03:28:49.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003979)  
for Clean Water Act violations.

**[03:28:51.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003980)  
Sa Monterey Park Data Center,

**[03:28:53.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003981)  
hyperscale facility ng EDGE Corps in California,

**[03:28:57.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003982)  
environmental colonialism daw,

**[03:28:59.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003983)  
hindi innovation,

**[03:29:00.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003984)  
and 3,000 signatures

**[03:29:01.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003985)  
and testimony of farm workers

**[03:29:03.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003986)  
na naapektuhan ng crop damaging runoff

**[03:29:05.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003987)  
ang nangyari.

**[03:29:06.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003988)  
I could go on,

**[03:29:07.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003989)  
pero saglit lang po yung oras ko.

**[03:29:08.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003990)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:29:09.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003991)  
ang punto naman po

**[03:29:10.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003992)  
ng ginagawa natin,

**[03:29:11.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003993)  
may valid concerns ang mamamayan.

**[03:29:13.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003994)  
Narinig din po natin sa DTI kahapon

**[03:29:16.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003995)  
na hindi po,

**[03:29:16.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003996)  
nila masabi na wala pong data center

**[03:29:20.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003997)  
na maaaring matayo sa New Clark City.

**[03:29:22.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003998)  
May manufacturing hub na iniisip

**[03:29:24.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003999)  
pero data center not off the table.

**[03:29:26.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004000)  
So, ako po,

**[03:29:27.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004001)  
kung may maisasumite po yung DNR

**[03:29:30.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004002)  
na information tungkol po

**[03:29:32.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004003)  
sa environmental impact

**[03:29:34.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004004)  
ng isang data center

**[03:29:36.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004005)  
ng manufacturing hub,

**[03:29:37.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004006)  
this representation would appreciate it po.

**[03:29:40.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004007)  
Sige, yun po muna

**[03:29:41.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004008)  
and siguro yung next question ko na lang po

**[03:29:43.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004009)  
nasimulan na po kanina

**[03:29:44.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004010)  
yung tungkol po sa

**[03:29:46.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004011)  
Executive

**[03:29:46.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004012)  
Order No. 122

**[03:29:48.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004013)  
which is

**[03:29:49.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004014)  
tungkol po sa Critical Minerals Framework ng Pilipinas.

**[03:29:53.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004015)  
So ang narinig po natin, according to this,

**[03:29:56.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004016)  
ay may 9 million hectares na prospective areas

**[03:30:02.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004017)  
with potential to hold various mineral resources.

**[03:30:05.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004018)  
So I'd like to ask po sa DNR and sa MGB

**[03:30:10.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004019)  
dahil po kasi sila yung tinitignan

**[03:30:13.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004020)  
na mag-implement ng National Exploration and Mineral Reservation Program

**[03:30:18.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004021)  
to identify and evaluate new critical mineral deposits.

**[03:30:21.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004022)  
Ano na pong mga aksyon or karampatang preliminary steps

**[03:30:26.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004023)  
para po i-implement na yung EO-122?

**[03:30:30.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004024)  
That's the last question, Sec. Will you please reply?

**[03:30:34.320] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-004025)  
Director Larry, please.

**[03:30:38.560] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004026)  
Yung ating exploration program po sa MGB,

**[03:30:43.660] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004027)  
continuing naman po yan.

**[03:30:45.860] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004028)  
Yung EO-122, as just highlighted po natin,

**[03:30:49.900] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004029)  
to have a unified framework on how to develop yung critical minerals.

**[03:30:55.880] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004030)  
But yung pong critical minerals, identified na po natin yan,

**[03:30:59.160] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004031)  
which is gold, copper, nickel,

**[03:31:01.660] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004032)  
na meron naman tayong data.

**[03:31:03.200] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004033)  
And we are continuing to explore and find out

**[03:31:06.580] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004034)  
where these minerals are located,

**[03:31:10.060] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004035)  
if they are mine-feasible ba yung pagmumina doon sa kanila,

**[03:31:15.710] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004036)  
yun po yung ginagawa ng MGB ngayon.

**[03:31:17.820] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004037)  
Yung critical minerals, highlights lang,

**[03:31:20.000] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004038)

**[03:31:23.920] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004039)

**[03:31:28.720] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004040)

**[03:31:30.100] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004041)  
And yung ibang lugar na ating ma-discover,

**[03:31:34.140] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004042)  
maaari po din natin i-declare na mineral reservation

**[03:31:36.820] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004043)  
because kung mineral reservation siya,

**[03:31:39.420] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004044)  
may added premium po ang government for the royalty payment po.

**[03:31:43.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-004045)  
If ever, that mineral will be extracted and utilized.

**[04:30:59.680] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005664)

**[04:31:10.220] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005666)

**[04:31:15.760] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005667)

**[04:31:18.340] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005668)

**[04:31:27.180] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005669)

**[04:31:30.240] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005670)

**[04:31:42.880] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005673)

**[04:31:44.820] Rep. Leonel "Jhong" D. Ceniza** (asr-005674)  
Province of Davao de Oro and the local government unit para makontrol po yung waste disposal ng mga small scale miners.

**[04:49:36.420] MGB Region V Regional Director Guillermo A. Molina Jr.** (asr-006029)

**[04:50:00.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006033)

**[04:50:11.360] MGB Region V Regional Director Guillermo A. Molina Jr.** (asr-006035)

**[04:50:21.920] MGB Region V Regional Director Guillermo A. Molina Jr.** (asr-006036)

**[04:50:31.360] MGB Region V Regional Director Guillermo A. Molina Jr.** (asr-006037)  
and therefore we have disallowed the disposal of these white clay deposits inside the scope

**[04:50:40.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006039)

**[04:50:44.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006041)

**[04:51:00.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006043)

**[04:51:01.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006044)  
Because there are reports,

**[04:54:56.640] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006129)  
Good afternoon to the DEA and our family, hit by our good Secretary.

**[04:55:03.420] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006130)

**[04:55:12.240] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006131)

**[04:55:16.320] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006132)  
First, Cordillera is one of the forested region entire the country.

**[04:55:24.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006133)  
I was listening to your budget for intensified forest protection.

**[04:55:31.060] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006134)

**[04:55:39.660] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006135)

**[04:55:49.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006136)

**[04:55:50.400] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006137)

**[04:56:00.940] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006139)

**[04:56:12.400] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006140)

**[04:56:18.020] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006141)

**[04:56:21.460] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006142)  
Thank you for your question, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:56:23.160] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006143)  
We do have specific budgets for that.

**[04:56:25.120] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-006144)

**[04:56:29.440] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006145)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[04:56:32.160] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006146)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:56:33.960] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006147)  
Under the National Greening Program, Mr. Chairman, we have development activities within protection

**[04:56:40.780] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006148)  
zone where we can undertake enrichment planting and assisted natural generation.

**[04:56:46.440] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006149)  
For 2027, we have targeted 16,000 hectares.

**[04:56:51.560] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006150)  
I mean, the unit cost is 16,000 per hectare.

**[04:56:57.060] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006151)  
So that's about 8,000 hectares or a budget of about 130 million, Mr. Chair.

**[04:57:03.580] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006152)  
We also have a separate budget also within protection zone for reforestation.

**[04:57:10.760] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006153)  
Within production zone, we have budget for agroforestry.

**[04:57:14.340] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006154)  
And for other areas that we mentioned, forest reserves, we do site planning and site protection and maintenance, Mr. Chair.

**[04:57:25.120] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006156)  
And we also conduct support activities to provide technical assistance, Mr. Chair.

**[04:57:32.660] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006157)  
Thank you, Madam.

**[04:57:36.500] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006158)

**[04:57:46.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006159)

**[04:57:51.060] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006160)  
On my next point, solid waste disposal system is a serious problem in entire the country.

**[04:57:59.840] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006161)

**[04:58:06.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006162)

**[04:58:13.920] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006163)

**[04:58:21.740] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006164)

**[04:58:28.140] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006165)

**[04:58:30.120] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006166)

**[04:58:38.380] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006167)

**[04:59:06.940] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-006172)

**[05:26:59.900] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006930)  
Mr. Chair, the mandate of the DNR pertaining to flood mitigation activities or projects

**[05:27:08.320] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006931)  
are within the activities of watershed management.

**[05:27:12.800] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006932)

**[05:27:20.100] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006933)  
So, that would also include the NGP for the reforestation of about 147 million.

**[05:27:45.220] DENR Undersecretary Analiza Rebuelta-Teh** (asr-006940)

**[05:30:23.620] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-006975)

**[05:31:40.720] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-006987)  
Okay, thank you, ma'am. Thank you, Mr. Chair. Another thing, sa Jensen, nung tumama yung magnitude 7.8 na lindol,

**[05:31:52.560] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-006989)  
napagalaman namin na a year before nangyari yun,

**[05:31:57.260] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-006990)  
nagsumite na ng report si PHIVOLCS sa LGU na kung saan merong 38% sa barangay ng GenSan may liquefaction.

**[05:32:08.880] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-006991)  
Yung concern ko sa MGB, yung sa geohazard mapping, paano siya ma-merge or ma-integrate yung liquefaction? Kasi with our experience, pag nagbigay yung PHIVOLCS doon sa LGU, hindi nami-merge yung data sa geohazard mapping nila. Hindi naka-layer. So, magkaiba yung data na natatanggap at saka yung sa geohazard mapping. Ngayon, paano siya makaka-comply yung mga bagong building permits at saka yung zoning ng CLUP? Dadalawa yung basis nila. Mr. Secretary.

**[05:33:03.960] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-007001)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[05:33:05.820] DENR Acting Secretary Juan Miguel T. Cuna** (asr-007002)  
We'd like to defer to Dr. Karlo. The assistant director of MGB.

**[05:33:11.460] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007004)  
Thank you, Secretary. I'd like to respond. As a geologist, we're implementing the Department Administrative Order 2000-28 in which all infrastructures, whether government or private, should conduct an engineering, geological, and geo-hazard assessment as an additional requirement for the issuance of the ECC.

**[05:33:34.160] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007010)  
So, essentially, before you construct a building, you should know the physical conditions, if it's liquefaction-prone, if it's flood-prone, or maybe if it's landslide-prone.

**[05:33:43.780] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007013)  
So, basically, you map out all of these geo-hazards. So, it's not just simply superimposing the liquefaction because liquefaction and volcanic-related hazards are the mandates of the Philippine Institute of Volcanology. But in the same way, identifying the faults, and that's also perform the fevals. But on the side of the MGB, DNR, that's flooding and landslide, as well as what we call the karst, and to a certain extent, coast.

**[05:34:08.660] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007019)  
But regardless of the mandate, it is important in the DNR admissive order that all of these hazards are looked into by the private entity.

**[05:34:17.240] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007021)  
So, the process is the private entity would hire a private geologist to produce an engineering, geological, and geo-hazard assessment, after which the DNR MGB would review that document as to whether the document is able to address this hazard and design a particular infrastructure according to the hazard.

**[05:34:38.080] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007026)  
So, it's not just simply mitigating, but also adapting the structure.

**[05:34:40.840] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007027)  
Okay. Mr. Chair, yung concern lang,

**[05:34:44.060] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007028)  
yung sa geo-hazard mapping ba ng DNR, kasama na ang liquefaction doon?

**[05:34:49.180] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007029)  
Hindi. Feebox yun?

**[05:34:51.640] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007030)  
Yes po. That's why po, in the geo-risk hazards hunter,

**[05:34:56.000] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007031)  
these maps are integrated so that the end user would be able to make use of all of these maps po.

**[05:35:01.160] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007032)  
Hindi kasi yung concern namin, nalimbawa sa DPWH, yung sabi ng DPWH, hindi magkakaroon, nang future, kailangan may concordance ng DNR, yung mga future projects, especially related to flood control. Pero kung hindi nakasama yung liquefaction map doon sa basis ni DNR, possible pa rin na may mangyayaring masama kung sakaling may liquefaction na hindi siya na-factor in doon.

**[05:35:33.620] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007040)  
Tama po yan. That's why po, importante po sa isang building, or whatever infrastructure that these hazards are integrated,

**[05:35:41.160] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007042)  
unfortunately po for Gensan, it is underlain, a large portion of it is underlain by Lahar.

**[05:35:46.820] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007044)  
That's the reason why kapag naglindolo, matindi po yung ground shaking po niya.

**[05:35:50.940] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007045)  
And also, liquefaction is one of those, what you call these consequences of strong ground shaking.

**[05:35:57.180] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007046)  
That's why po, sa mga ganyang klaseng mga lugar, importante yung na-integrate yan sa design.

**[05:36:02.980] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007048)  
I mean to say, yung liquefiable, for example, na layer, ay bina-bypass po ng foundation ng isang building mo.

**[05:36:08.500] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007050)  
But I believe po, because of the DNR business order, that is actually a requirement for all these private entities to integrate all of these hazards before implementing that project po.

**[05:36:20.180] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007053)  
Mr. Chair, yung concern ko, Mr. Chair, kung may way ba na ma-merge yung data na yan,

**[05:36:30.860] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007054)  
DNR, ma-merge yung data na yan para pag nagbigay ng certification or concurrency DNR, kasama na yun, na-consider na yung ano, kasama yung data, nang liquefaction.

**[05:36:46.320] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007057)  
May I reply po?

**[05:36:47.500] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007058)  
Actually po, the municipalities at lahat po ng mga provincial, they can actually superimpose all of these maps.

**[05:36:55.180] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007060)  
Dahil lang po yan ay available po nga po sa Hazards Hunter.

**[05:36:58.480] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007061)  
But of course, on the part of the DNR MGB, we are also offer our assistance to review all of the CLUP that also integrates this kind of hazard.

**[05:37:08.500] MGB Assistant Director Karlo L. Queaño** (asr-007063)  
So as to be able to all these local governments to understand, and how to actually make use of those maps in the context of their land use plans.

**[05:37:17.580] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-007065)  
Okay, that's all, Mr. Chair. Thank you.

**[06:31:09.440] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008943)

**[06:31:20.140] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008944)

**[06:31:45.680] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008946)

**[06:31:57.540] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008947)

**[06:32:07.300] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008948)  
Mr. Chair, may I?

**[06:32:08.540] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008949)

**[06:32:11.440] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008950)

**[06:32:23.080] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008951)

**[06:32:27.080] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008952)

**[06:32:39.560] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008953)

**[06:32:42.500] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008954)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[06:32:44.300] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008955)

**[06:33:01.300] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008956)

**[06:33:09.160] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008957)

**[06:33:23.060] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008958)

**[06:33:30.660] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008959)

**[06:33:35.420] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008960)

**[06:33:37.540] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008962)  
As of this stage,

**[06:33:39.540] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008963)

**[06:33:44.960] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008964)

**[06:33:52.540] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008965)

**[06:34:01.080] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008966)

**[06:34:07.980] Rep. Agatha Paula "Agay" A. Cruz** (asr-008968)

**[06:34:14.360] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008969)

**[06:34:15.600] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008970)

**[06:34:31.060] NWRB Executive Director Sevillo D. David Jr.** (asr-008972)

**[06:37:38.830] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-009008)

**[06:37:44.720] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-009009)

**[06:37:58.380] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-009012)  
Mr. Chair, Secretary, We spend billions of pesos every year on flood control infrastructure. But if we do not protect the forests, watersheds, rivers, and wetlands that naturally absorb and regulate water, we may simply be addressing the symptoms rather than the root cause of flooding.

**[06:38:20.740] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-009016)

**[06:38:33.980] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-009017)

**[06:43:02.840] FMB Director Arturo E. Fadriquela** (asr-009062)  
Meron po tayong definition, working definition ng critical watershed. Ang sinasabi po, it is based on PD 705. It is a drainage area of a river system supplying existing and proposed hydroelectric power, irrigation works, and domestic water facilities needing immediate protection and rehabilitation. So yung mga identified watershed, yung mga watershed na bumabagsak po sa definition na ito, na supporting existing and proposed hydroelectric power, irrigation works, and domestic water utilities, ay nakaklasify po natin siya as critical watershed.

natin ay high economic importance,

**[07:19:36.000] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009711)

**[07:20:05.600] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009716)

**[07:20:08.920] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009717)

**[07:29:00.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009940)

**[07:29:02.920] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009941)  
ka-JV partner niya

**[07:29:04.080] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009942)  
right now. So,

**[07:29:06.120] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009943)  
inaasikaso namin yun, may monitoring

**[07:29:08.900] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009944)

**[07:29:11.480] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009945)  
hindi nabigay noon,

**[07:29:13.540] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009946)  
eh, tinututukan namin mabigay

**[07:29:15.340] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009947)  
ngayon, sir.

**[07:29:16.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009948)  
In cases of disputes, ano po yung

**[07:29:18.540] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009949)  
role ng LUA?

**[07:29:20.740] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009950)  
Sa cases of disputes po, the Public Service Act

**[07:29:23.020] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009951)  
does not give us quasi-judicial

**[07:29:25.420] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009952)  
powers over that. So,

**[07:29:26.860] LWUA Acting Administrator Jose Moises F. Salonga** (asr-009953)  
again, that is an issue, sir.

**[07:29:31.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009954)  
Thank you very much. Thank you.

**[07:29:33.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009956)  
In the video water act, merong quasi-judicial power.

**[07:29:36.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009956-split-1)

**[07:30:01.000] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009962)  
To the MGB Director, Director Heradez,

**[07:30:07.640] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009963)  
I will just direct my question with the permission of our Chair.

**[07:30:12.100] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009964)  
I will just direct my question to the MGB Director, Director Heradez.

**[07:30:21.000] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009965)  
Kilala niyo po ba ang Mount Sinai mining sa Homonhon, pa rin po ito?

**[07:30:29.620] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009966)  
Mount Sinai.

**[07:30:30.860] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009967)  
Mount Sinai po, opo. Yes po.

**[07:30:33.400] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009968)  
May I ask, ano po ang status nito ngayon?

**[07:30:37.240] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009969)  
Ang Mount Sinai po ay meron ding approved MPSA number 351-211.

**[07:30:45.420] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009970)  
Na-approve po siya noong 2011.

**[07:30:47.880] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009971)  
But na-approve po din po yung Declaration of Mining Project Feasibility ngayon noong 2011.

**[07:30:56.700] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009972)  
However, since 2013, nasa under care and maintenance po ang MPSA.

**[07:31:06.300] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009973)  
Salamat, Director.

**[07:31:07.660] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009974)  
Second question.

**[07:31:11.200] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009975)  
Balik po tayo sa Cambayas Mining.

**[07:31:13.500] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009976)  
Kilala niyo po ba yung may-ari ng Cambayas Mining?

**[07:31:17.240] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009977)  
Lahat ito sa Homonhon, ha?

**[07:31:18.480] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009978)  
Lahat po ito, Mr. Chair.

**[07:31:20.880] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009979)  
Sa Homonhon ito?

**[07:31:23.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009981)  
Subject matter para bukas.

**[07:31:24.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009982)  
Dalhin mo na sa kanya lahat.

**[07:31:26.340] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009983)  
Ang Cambayas Mining po, sila po yung mga naunang mining company na nag-operate po for chromite sa Homonhon.

**[07:31:36.460] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009984)  
Do you have any knowledge sino may-ari nito, Director?

**[07:31:40.480] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009985)  
Meron po.

**[07:31:41.180] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009986)  
Ang may-ari po niyan is the late engineer.

**[07:31:44.680] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009987)  
A certain Casido. Engineer Casido.

**[07:31:47.220] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009988)  
Are you sure of this po?

**[07:31:49.560] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-009989)  
Yes po, as per our record po.

**[07:31:51.880] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009990)  
Okay.

**[07:31:53.360] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009991)  
Under cambayas, as I have said a while ago, in-split, naging dalawa.

**[07:31:59.960] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009992)  
Ang tanong ko po dito, nung in-split po siya, naging dalawa yung MPSA.

**[07:32:04.880] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009993)  
May amended A, amended B.

**[07:32:08.200] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009994)  
Bakit po nagkaroon ng tatlong operators?

**[07:32:13.260] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009995)  
Operator ng agreement sa isang MPSA.

**[07:32:16.740] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009996)  
Do it kami.

**[07:32:18.220] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-009997)  
Tatlong operator sa isang MPSA.

**[07:32:21.230] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009998)  
Larry? Pwede ba yan?

**[07:32:27.680] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010001)  
Nung mga naunang implementation po natin when the mining act was issued po, we allowed po yung ganong system.

**[07:32:36.960] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010002)  
We can approve a number of operating agreements po from one MPSA.

**[07:32:43.460] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010003)  
However, nagkaroon na nga po ng mga problems with regards to a single MPSA operated by several or a number of operators.

**[07:32:54.220] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010004)  
Nagka-problema po.

**[07:32:55.340] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010005)  
Kaya po nung 2021 or 2022, we issued a policy of prohibiting more than one.

**[07:33:04.980] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010006)  
One operator, one MPSA po ang nilabas ng DENR.

**[07:33:09.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-010007)  
Very good.

**[07:33:10.212] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010008)  
Siguro, alam nyo na po yung sinasabi ko sa inyo, Director, kahit saan po natin tingnan ito sa batas, wala po tayong makikita na pwede po yan.

**[07:33:27.580] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010011)  
Anyway, Section 96 of Republic Act 7942, also known as the Mining Act.

**[07:33:35.840] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010012)  
I will read.

**[07:33:37.880] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010013)  
Violation of the terms and condition of the permits or agreements shall be a sufficient ground for cancellation of the same.

**[07:33:46.180] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010014)  
Ang ganito pong pagkakataon, nakikita niyo, siyempre nasa inyo naman po yung bola, kayo yung mag-iimbestiga, kayo yung makakakita sa ground,

**[07:33:58.160] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010015)  
as I have said kanina, na pwedeng puntahan nyo doon.

**[07:34:01.860] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010016)  
Ano po ang maikukumit nyo dito?

**[07:34:08.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-010017)  
Larry?

**[07:34:09.440] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010018)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[07:34:11.220] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010019)  
Kahapon po, nag-meeting po yung tungkol sa Homonhon mining operation.

**[07:34:15.980] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010020)  
Ang agreement po ay we will conduct a general assessment of the mining operation po.

**[07:34:23.600] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010021)  
Gawin naman ninyong rapid?

**[07:34:24.860] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010023)  
Kasi kung assessment mo isang taon.

**[07:34:27.060] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010024)  
Rapid po. Mabilis po na assessment.

**[07:34:30.760] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010025)  
Then we commit that after that assessment immediately, we will have a concrete action on the situation po doon.

**[07:34:38.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-010026)

**[07:34:44.440] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010027)  
Mr. Chair.

**[07:34:55.660] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010029)

**[07:35:01.980] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-010030)

**[07:35:06.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-010031)

**[07:35:10.980] MGB Director Larry M. Heradez** (asr-010033)

I will commit that immediate
